### [1] [Marginal Gains or Meaningful Progress? Exploring Tech Tuber Narratives on Annual Smartphone Innovation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02392)
*Chandima Wickramatunga,Ruwan Nagahawatta,Anagi Gamachchi,Chintha Kaluarachchi*

Main category: cs.ET

TL;DR: 专家分析显示智能手机年度更新缺乏实质性创新，多为渐进式改进，难以证明频繁升级的合理性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究智能手机制造商每年发布新机型但创新步伐放缓的现象，探究这些更新是否真正为用户带来可感知的益处

Method: 采用扎根理论方法，以罗杰斯创新扩散理论为指导框架，分析2021-2025年间三位科技评论员的评论话语

Result: 识别出维持创新感知的三个相互关联过程：创新转移、能力效用分歧和市场自满循环；改进虽被承认但不足以证明年度发布的合理性

Conclusion: 行业创新叙事与专家价值评估之间存在日益扩大的脱节，对频繁升级的战略和环境合理性提出质疑，为负责任创新、感知价值和可持续技术消费的讨论做出贡献

Abstract: Smartphone manufacturers continue to release new models annually, yet the pace of meaningful innovation has slowed, with most changes limited to incremental updates in design, performance, or software. This study examines whether such updates deliver tangible user benefits, as perceived by expert reviewers. Using a grounded theory approach, guided by Rogers Diffusion of Innovation (DOI) framework, the research analyses reviewer discourse from 2021 2025 across three technology commentators. The analysis identifies three interrelated processes sustaining perceptions of innovation: innovation displacement, capability utility divergence, and market complacency cycles. While some improvements are acknowledged, such as refined aesthetics or extended software support; they are seldom judged sufficient to justify annual releases. These findings highlight a growing disconnect between industry narratives of innovation and expert evaluations of value, raising questions about the strategic and environmental legitimacy of frequent upgrades. The study contributes to debates on responsible innovation, perceived value, and sustainable technology consumption.

</details>

### [2] [Silent Sabotage During Fine-Tuning: Few-Shot Rationale Poisoning of Compact Medical LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02262)
*Jingyuan Xie,Wenjie Wang,Ji Wu,Jiandong Gao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出针对医疗大语言模型监督微调阶段的新型推理过程投毒攻击，通过向少样本训练数据注入有毒推理过程，实现对特定医学主题性能的隐蔽降级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有投毒研究主要关注可检测的后门攻击，而医疗大语言模型的监督微调阶段存在安全风险，需要探索更隐蔽的攻击方式。

Method: 在少样本训练数据中注入有毒推理过程（rationale poisoning），而非传统的知识覆盖，通过污染推理路径来影响模型对特定医学主题的判断能力。

Result: 推理过程投毒能显著降低模型在目标医学主题上的准确性，只要数据集中不出现同一主题的正确样本；相比灾难性遗忘，该方法更高效准确；需要最小数量和比例的投毒样本才能实现有效且隐蔽的攻击。

Conclusion: 监督微调阶段存在推理过程投毒风险，这种隐蔽攻击对敏感的医疗领域构成威胁，需要更多防御研究来应对此类安全挑战。

Abstract: Supervised fine-tuning (SFT) is essential for the development of medical large language models (LLMs), yet prior poisoning studies have mainly focused on the detectable backdoor attacks. We propose a novel poisoning attack targeting the reasoning process of medical LLMs during SFT. Unlike backdoor attacks, our method injects poisoned rationales into few-shot training data, leading to stealthy degradation of model performance on targeted medical topics. Results showed that knowledge overwriting was ineffective, while rationale poisoning caused significant decline on the accuracy of the target subject, as long as no correct samples of the same subject appear in the dataset. A minimum number and ratio of poisoned samples was needed to carry out an effective and stealthy attack, which was more efficient and accurate than catastrophic forgetting. We demonstrate though this study the risk of SFT-stage poisoning, hoping to spur more studies of defense in the sensitive medical domain.

</details>


### [3] [Authenticated Contradictions from Desynchronized Provenance and Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02378)
*Alexander Nemecek,Hengzhi He,Guang Cheng,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文发现C2PA加密溯源标准与隐形水印在内容认证中存在"完整性冲突"：数字资产可同时携带有效C2PA声明（声称人为创作）和AI生成水印，两者独立验证均能通过。作者提出跨层审计协议解决此问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C2PA加密溯源标准和隐形水印被视为内容认证的互补防御措施，但两个验证层在技术上相互独立，互不依赖对方的输出结果。这种独立性可能导致验证漏洞，需要研究两者间的潜在冲突。

Method: 1. 形式化定义"完整性冲突"概念；2. 构建元数据清洗工作流，通过标准编辑管道生成经过认证的伪造内容；3. 设计跨层审计协议，联合评估溯源元数据和水印检测状态；4. 在3,500张测试图像上进行实验，涵盖四种冲突矩阵状态和三种现实扰动条件。

Result: 1. 成功演示了完整性冲突的存在；2. 元数据清洗工作流无需密码学妥协，仅需省略C2PA规范允许的单个声明字段；3. 跨层审计协议在测试中实现了100%的分类准确率；4. 证明两个验证层之间的差距是不必要的，且技术上易于弥合。

Conclusion: C2PA和隐形水印作为独立验证层存在完整性冲突漏洞，但通过跨层审计协议可以解决这一问题。研究结果表明，两个验证层之间的技术差距是不必要的，可以通过联合评估机制有效弥合。

Abstract: Cryptographic provenance standards such as C2PA and invisible watermarking are positioned as complementary defenses for content authentication, yet the two verification layers are technically independent: neither conditions on the output of the other. This work formalizes and empirically demonstrates the $\textit{Integrity Clash}$, a condition in which a digital asset carries a cryptographically valid C2PA manifest asserting human authorship while its pixels simultaneously carry a watermark identifying it as AI-generated, with both signals passing their respective verification checks in isolation. We construct metadata washing workflows that produce these authenticated fakes through standard editing pipelines, requiring no cryptographic compromise, only the semantic omission of a single assertion field permitted by the current C2PA specification. To close this gap, we propose a cross-layer audit protocol that jointly evaluates provenance metadata and watermark detection status, achieving 100% classification accuracy across 3,500 test images spanning four conflict-matrix states and three realistic perturbation conditions. Our results demonstrate that the gap between these verification layers is unnecessary and technically straightforward to close.

</details>


### [4] [TraceGuard: Process-Guided Firewall against Reasoning Backdoors in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02436)
*Zhen Guo,Shanghao Shi,Hao Li,Shamim Yazdani,Ning Zhang,Reza Tourani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TraceGuard是一个针对大型推理模型中间推理链的安全框架，通过三阶段防御策略将小型模型转化为强大的推理防火墙，有效检测推理后门攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在关键决策中的部署引入了新的攻击面——推理后门攻击，攻击者操纵模型的中间推理链，使其提供语言上合理但逻辑错误的论证来支持恶意结论。前沿模型具有内在的检测能力，但紧凑、可部署的模型存在验证缺口，依赖脆弱的词汇启发式方法，容易被攻击者绕过。

Method: TraceGuard采用三层防御策略：1) 自动法证合成，生成对比推理对以隔离逻辑断裂点；2) 步骤感知的监督微调，建立结构化验证语法；3) 验证器引导的强化学习，使用组相对策略优化。该方法解决了基线对齐中的词汇过拟合问题。

Result: 4B参数的验证器在未见过的攻击（包括潜在后门和事后合理化）上实现了法证级精度，性能可与大两个数量级的架构相媲美。在灰盒设置中对自适应攻击者表现出鲁棒性，成为可信计算基的低延迟安全原语。

Conclusion: TraceGuard作为一个安全力倍增器，填补了紧凑模型的推理验证缺口，通过过程引导的安全框架将小型模型转化为强大的推理防火墙，为可信计算基提供了可行、低延迟的安全原语。

Abstract: The deployment of Large Reasoning Models (LRMs) in high-stakes decision-making pipelines has introduced a novel and opaque attack surface: reasoning backdoors. In these attacks, the model's intermediate Chain-of-Thought (CoT) is manipulated to provide a linguistically plausible but logically fallacious justification for a malicious conclusion. While frontier models exhibit an intrinsic capacity to detect these fractures, compact, deployable models suffer from a fundamental verification gap, relying on fragile lexical heuristics that are easily bypassed by motivated adversaries.
  To bridge this gap, we propose TraceGuard, a process-guided security framework that transforms small-scale models into robust reasoning firewalls. Our approach treats the reasoning trace as an untrusted payload and establishes a defense-in-depth strategy through three synergistic phases: (1) Automated Forensic Synthesis, which generates contrastive reasoning pairs to isolate the specific logical point of fracture; (2) Step-Aware Supervised Fine-Tuning (SSFT), to instill a structural verification grammar; and (3) Verifier-Guided Reinforcement Learning (VGRL), utilizing Group Relative Policy Optimization. We identify and mitigate a critical failure mode of baseline alignment - lexical overfitting - whereby verifiers memorize adversarial triggers rather than auditing logical integrity. Our empirical evaluation demonstrates that TraceGuard acts as a security force multiplier: a 4B-parameter verifier achieves forensic precision on unseen attacks - including latent backdoors and post-hoc rationalizations - that rivals architectures two orders of magnitude larger. We further demonstrate robustness against adaptive adversaries in a grey-box setting, establishing TraceGuard as a viable, low-latency security primitive for the Trusted Computing Base.

</details>


### [5] [Composable Attestation: A Generalized Framework for Continuous and Incremental Trust in AI-Driven Distributed Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02451)
*Sheng Sun,Sarah Evans*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出可组合证明作为分布式系统中连续增量信任的通用密码学框架，支持AI计算和开源软件供应链验证，基于Merkle树等密码结构实现模块化、可扩展的完整性验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统证明方法采用整体验证，难以适应动态变化的分布式系统配置。需要建立模块化、可扩展的密码学完整性验证框架，以支持AI计算、开源软件供应链等复杂分布式环境中的连续增量信任。

Method: 建立严格的数学基础，定义可组合证明系统的核心属性：可组合性、顺序无关性、传递性、确定性、包含性和动态组件验证。提出通用证明生成和验证函数，可通过多种密码结构实现，其中Merkle树在构建可组合证明中起关键作用。同时探索累加器方案和多签名方法等替代构造。

Result: 形式化分析表明这些实现遵循基本属性。框架可应用于安全AI模型完整性验证、联邦学习和运行时信任保证。引入证明包含概念，允许新组件增量集成而无需完整系统重新证明。

Conclusion: 可组合证明框架通过密码学可验证证明机制增强AI计算和更广泛分布式计算环境中的信任，建立在信任引导的基础概念之上，为动态分布式系统提供模块化、可扩展的完整性验证解决方案。

Abstract: This paper presents composable attestation as a generalized cryptographic framework for Continuous and Incremental Trust in Distributed Systems,such as Artificial Intelligence (AI) computation, and Open Source Software (OSS) supply chain verification. We establish a rigorous mathematical foundation which is defining core properties of such attestation systems: composability, order independence, transitivity, determinism, inclusion, and dynamic component verification. In contrast to traditional attestation methodologies relying on monolithic verification, composable attestation facilitates modular, scalable, and cryptographically secured integrity verification adaptable to evolving system configurations. This work introduces generalized attestation proof generation and verification functions, implementable via a variety of cryptographic constructions, in which Merkle trees plays vital role in constructing the composable attestation proof. Alternative constructions, including accumulator-based schemes and multi-signature approaches, are also explored, each presenting distinct trade-offs in performance, security, and functionality. Formal analysis demonstrates the adherence of these implementations to the fundamental properties . The framework's utility extends to applications such as secure AI model integrity verification , federated learning, and runtime trust assurance. The concept of attestation inclusion is introduced, permitting incremental integration of new components without necessitating full system re-attestation. This generalized approach reinforce trust in AI computation and broader distributed computing environments through cryptographically verifiable proof mechanisms, building upon foundational concepts of bootstrapping trust.

</details>


### [6] [Exploiting PendingIntent Provenance Confusion to Spoof Android SDK Authentication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02539)
*Ramanpreet Singh Khinda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 研究发现Android跨应用SDK的身份验证机制存在根本性漏洞，攻击者可通过NotificationListenerService窃取PendingIntent并重放，完全绕过身份验证，影响数百万应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OWASP 2024移动安全十大风险将供应链安全不足列为第二大关键风险，第三方SDK被明确识别为主要攻击向量。跨应用SDK通过进程间通信（IPC）处理敏感操作，但现有身份验证机制存在根本性缺陷，可能导致整个合作伙伴生态系统的身份被盗用。

Method: 系统评估了八种Android IPC身份验证机制，针对SDK特定威胁模型进行分析。演示了PendingIntent.getCreatorPackage()身份验证机制的根本性来源混淆漏洞：攻击者应用通过NotificationListenerService窃取合法合作伙伴的PendingIntent并重放，完全绕过身份验证。该攻击对可变和不可变PendingIntent均有效。

Result: 发现PendingIntent身份验证机制存在根本性漏洞，攻击者可通过通知访问权限窃取并重放PendingIntent，完全绕过身份验证。提出了一种防御架构：结合Bound Service IPC与通过Binder.getCallingUid()的内核级调用者验证，辅以服务器端证书哈希验证，提供可扩展的身份验证保证。

Conclusion: 跨应用SDK的PendingIntent身份验证机制存在根本性来源混淆漏洞，攻击者可窃取并重放PendingIntent完全绕过身份验证。提出的防御架构结合Bound Service IPC、内核级调用者验证和服务器端验证，为合作伙伴生态系统提供可扩展的安全身份验证解决方案。

Abstract: A single authentication bypass in a partner SDK grants attackers the identity of every partner in the ecosystem -- and millions of apps use SDKs with exactly this vulnerability. OWASP's 2024 Mobile Top 10 ranks Inadequate Supply Chain Security as the second most critical mobile risk, explicitly identifying third-party SDKs as a primary attack vector. Cross-app mobile SDKs -- where a partner application communicates with a platform provider's application via inter-process communication (IPC) -- mediate sensitive operations such as content publishing, payment initiation, and identity federation. Unlike embedded libraries that execute within a single app's process, cross-app SDKs require the provider's service to authenticate the calling application at runtime. A pattern sometimes used for this authentication relies on PendingIntent.getCreatorPackage() to verify sender identity. We demonstrate that this mechanism exhibits a fundamental provenance confusion vulnerability: a PendingIntent reliably identifies who created it but cannot attest who presents it -- and this distinction is fatal for authentication. An attacker app with notification access can steal a legitimate partner's PendingIntent via NotificationListenerService and replay it to impersonate that partner, bypassing authentication entirely. The attack succeeds against both mutable and immutable PendingIntents because immutability protects the token's contents, not its provenance. We systematically evaluate eight Android IPC authentication mechanisms against an SDK-specific threat model and present a defense architecture combining Bound Service IPC with kernel-level caller verification via Binder.getCallingUid(), supplemented by server-side certificate-hash validation. This provides authentication guarantees while remaining scalable across partner ecosystems.

</details>


### [7] [Blockchain Communication Vulnerabilities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02661)
*Andrei Lebedev,Vincent Gramoli*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 比较五种生产级区块链通信协议在五种攻击方式下的脆弱性，发现各区块链存在不同的安全漏洞


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链通信协议在节点间信息传播、攻击缓解和区块共识方面存在差异，但现有安全漏洞研究多针对特定协议，缺乏对不同区块链通信协议在对抗条件下的脆弱性比较

Method: 对五种现代生产级区块链（Algorand、Aptos、Avalanche、Redbelly、Solana）的通信协议进行实证比较，测试五种不同类型的攻击方式

Result: Algorand易受丢包攻击；Aptos易受定向负载攻击和领导者隔离攻击；Avalanche易受瞬态故障攻击；Redbelly性能受丢包攻击影响；Solana易受停止攻击和领导者隔离攻击

Conclusion: 不同区块链通信协议对不同类型的攻击表现出不同的脆弱性，需要针对特定协议设计相应的安全防护措施，研究系统已开源

Abstract: Blockchains are diverse in the way they handle communications between their nodes to disseminate information, mitigate attacks, and agree on the next block. While security vulnerabilities have been identified, they rely on an attack custom-made for a specific blockchain communication protocol. To our knowledge, the vulnerabilities of multiple blockchain communication protocols to adversarial conditions have never been compared.
  In this paper, we compare empirically the vulnerabilities of the communication protocols of five modern in-production blockchains, Algorand, Aptos, Avalanche, Redbelly and Solana, when attacked in five different ways. We conclude that Algorand is vulnerable to packet loss attacks, Aptos is vulnerable to targeted load attacks and leader isolation attacks, Avalanche is vulnerable to transient failure attacks, Redbelly's performance is impacted by packet loss attacks and Solana is vulnerable to stopping attacks and leader isolation attacks. Our system is open source.

</details>


### [8] [VA-DAR: A PQC-Ready, Vendor-Agnostic Deterministic Artifact Resolution for Serverless, Enumeration-Resistant Wallet Recovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02690)
*Jian Sheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VA-DAR是一种基于ACE-GF的无服务器钱包恢复协议，通过密钥发现机制实现跨设备恢复，仅需用户标识符和恢复密码，同时保护隐私并抵抗枚举攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无服务器钱包恢复需要在便携性、可用性和隐私之间取得平衡。公共注册表支持去中心化查找，但简单的标识符哈希会通过枚举泄露成员信息。需要一种既能实现跨设备恢复，又能保护隐私并抵抗枚举攻击的解决方案。

Method: VA-DAR作为ACE-GF之上的发现和恢复层，使用密码根密钥材料，通过内存硬KDF派生密钥，域分离用于工件密封和发现索引的密钥。发布由密码派生的发现标识符键控的注册表记录。协议包括三个密封工件角色、两种更新授权选项和三个协议流程（注册、恢复、更新）。

Result: VA-DAR提供：(1)仅使用标识符和密码的实用跨设备恢复；(2)对公共目录枚举的计算抵抗；(3)通过所有者授权保证发现映射的完整性；(4)通过单调版本控制和工件承诺实现回滚/篡改检测。在标准假设下证明满足安全目标。

Conclusion: VA-DAR为ACE-GF钱包提供了一个去中心化的发现和恢复层，平衡了便携性、可用性和隐私，支持后量子算法迁移，并通过形式化安全证明验证了其安全性。

Abstract: Serverless wallet recovery must balance portability, usability, and privacy. Public registries enable decentralized lookup but naive identifier hashing leaks membership through enumeration. We present VA-DAR, a keyed-discovery protocol for ACE-GF-based wallets that use device-bound passkeys for day-to-day local unlock while supporting cross-device recovery using only a user-provided identifier (e.g., email) and a single recovery passphrase. As a discovery-and-recovery layer over ACE-GF, VA-DAR inherits ACE-GF's context-isolated, algorithm-agile derivation substrate, enabling non-disruptive migration to post-quantum algorithms at the identity layer. The design introduces a decentralized discovery-and-recovery layer that maps a privacy-preserving discovery identifier to an immutable content identifier of a backup sealed artifact stored on a decentralized storage network. Concretely, a user derives passphrase-rooted key material with a memory-hard KDF, domain-separates keys for artifact sealing and discovery indexing, and publishes a registry record keyed by a passphrase-derived discovery identifier. VA-DAR provides: (i) practical cross-device recovery using only identifier and passphrase, (ii) computational resistance to public-directory enumeration, (iii) integrity of discovery mappings via owner authorization, and (iv) rollback/tamper detection via monotonic versioning and artifact commitments. We define three sealed artifact roles, two update-authorization options, and three protocol flows (registration, recovery, update). We formalize security goals via cryptographic games and prove, under standard assumptions, that VA-DAR meets these goals while remaining vendor-agnostic and chain-agnostic. End-to-end post-quantum deployment additionally requires a PQ-secure instantiation of registry authorization.

</details>


### [9] [Scores Know Bobs Voice: Speaker Impersonation Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02781)
*Chanwoo Hwang,Sunpill Kim,Yong Kiam Tan,Tianchi Liu,Seunghun Paik,Dongsoo Kim,Mondal Soumik,Khin Mi Mi Aung,Jae Hong Seo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于特征对齐反转的生成式攻击框架，通过将合成模型的潜在空间与说话人识别系统的判别特征空间对齐，显著提高了基于分数的冒充攻击的查询效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于原始波形的攻击需要大量查询，而生成模型的潜在空间优化虽然效率更高，但这些潜在空间由数据分布匹配形成，并未固有地捕捉说话人判别几何结构，导致优化轨迹往往无法与最大化受害者分数所需的对立方向对齐。

Method: 提出了一个反转基生成攻击框架，明确将合成模型的潜在空间与说话人识别系统的判别特征空间对齐。首先分析了基于分数攻击的反转模型需求，引入了特征对齐反转策略，在几何上同步潜在表示与说话人嵌入。这种对齐确保潜在更新直接转化为分数提升，并实现了新的攻击范式，包括基于子空间投影的攻击。

Result: 实验表明该方法显著提高了查询效率，以平均比先前方法少10倍的查询次数实现了竞争性的攻击成功率。特别是，实现的基于子空间投影攻击仅用50次查询就达到了91.65%的成功率。

Conclusion: 特征对齐反转是评估现代说话人识别系统对基于分数冒充威胁鲁棒性的关键工具，为高效对抗攻击提供了新范式。

Abstract: Advances in deep learning have enabled the widespread deployment of speaker recognition systems (SRSs), yet they remain vulnerable to score-based impersonation attacks. Existing attacks that operate directly on raw waveforms require a large number of queries due to the difficulty of optimizing in high-dimensional audio spaces. Latent-space optimization within generative models offers improved efficiency, but these latent spaces are shaped by data distribution matching and do not inherently capture speaker-discriminative geometry. As a result, optimization trajectories often fail to align with the adversarial direction needed to maximize victim scores.
  To address this limitation, we propose an inversion-based generative attack framework that explicitly aligns the latent space of the synthesis model with the discriminative feature space of SRSs. We first analyze the requirements of an inverse model for score-based attacks and introduce a feature-aligned inversion strategy that geometrically synchronizes latent representations with speaker embeddings. This alignment ensures that latent updates directly translate into score improvements. Moreover, it enables new attack paradigms, including subspace-projection-based attacks, which were previously infeasible due to the absence of a faithful feature-to-audio mapping.
  Experiments show that our method significantly improves query efficiency, achieving competitive attack success rates with on average 10x fewer queries than prior approaches. In particular, the enabled subspace-projection-based attack attains up to 91.65% success using only 50 queries. These findings establish feature-aligned inversion as a key tool for evaluating the robustness of modern SRSs against score-based impersonation threats.

</details>


### [10] [DSBA: Dynamic Stealthy Backdoor Attack with Collaborative Optimization in Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02849)
*Jiayao Wang,Mohammad Maruf Hasan,Yiping Zhang,Xiaoying Lei,Jiale Zhang,Qilin Wu,Junwu Zhu,Dongfang Zhao*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种针对自监督学习的动态隐蔽后门攻击方法DSBA，通过协作优化和动态触发器生成，在保持模型核心功能的同时提高攻击成功率和隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自监督学习虽然具有无需大量标注数据、泛化能力强和隐私保护等优势，但现有研究表明SSL模型同样容易受到后门攻击。当前SSL环境中的后门攻击方法存在触发器易被检测、特征纠缠以及中毒样本分布异常等问题，影响了攻击效果和隐蔽性。

Method: 提出动态隐蔽后门攻击(DSBA)，采用协作优化技术将攻击过程解耦为两层：外层优化训练负责全局特征空间重构的后门编码器，实现精确后门植入同时保持核心功能；内层优化使用动态优化生成器为每个样本自适应生成最优隐蔽触发器，实现特征空间和视觉空间的协同隐蔽。引入多种损失函数动态平衡攻击性能和隐蔽性，并采用自适应权重调度机制增强训练稳定性。

Result: 在多种主流SSL算法和五个公开数据集上的实验表明：(1) DSBA显著提高了攻击成功率和隐蔽性，同时保持了下游任务准确性；(2) DSBA对现有主流防御方法表现出优越的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DSBA通过协作优化和动态触发器生成，有效解决了SSL后门攻击中触发器易检测、特征纠缠和分布异常等问题，实现了高成功率、高隐蔽性且对防御方法具有鲁棒性的后门攻击。

Abstract: Self-Supervised Learning (SSL) has emerged as a significant paradigm in representation learning thanks to its ability to learn without extensive labeled data, its strong generalization capabilities, and its potential for privacy preservation. However, recent research reveals that SSL models are also vulnerable to backdoor attacks. Existing backdoor attack methods in the SSL context commonly suffer from issues such as high detectability of triggers, feature entanglement, and pronounced out-of-distribution properties in poisoned samples, all of which compromises attack effectiveness and stealthiness. To that, we propose a Dynamic Stealthy Backdoor Attack (DSBA) backed by a new technique we term Collaborative Optimization. This method decouples the attack process into two collaborative optimization layers: the outer-layer optimization trains a backdoor encoder responsible for global feature space remodeling, aiming to achieve precise backdoor implantation while preserving core functionality; meanwhile, the inner-layer optimization employs a dynamically optimized generator to adaptively produce optimally concealed triggers for individual samples, achieving coordinated concealment across feature space and visual space. We also introduce multiple loss functions to dynamically balance attack performance and stealthiness, in which we employ an adaptive weight scheduling mechanism to enhance training stability. Extensive experiments on various mainstream SSL algorithms and five public datasets demonstrate that: (i) DSBA significantly enhances Attack Success Rate (ASR) and stealthiness while maintaining downstream task accuracy; and (ii) DSBA exhibits superior robustness against existing mainstream defense methods.

</details>


### [11] [Kraken: Higher-order EM Side-Channel Attacks on DNNs in Near and Far Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02891)
*Peter Horvath,Ilia Shumailov,Lukasz Chmielewski,Lejla Batina,Yuval Yarom*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 首次通过近场物理侧信道攻击从GPU的Tensor Core单元提取参数，针对现代最常用的GPU单元，而非传统的CUDA Cores


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代机器学习模型投资巨大，成为窃取目标。现有基于物理侧信道的攻击仅针对GPU的通用CUDA Cores，而现代GPU最常用的是性能更优的Tensor Core单元，需要研究针对这些专用单元的模型窃取方法

Method: 针对GPU架构定制方法，精确估计能耗并通过相关性功耗分析（CPA）实施高效攻击；同时探索远场条件下LLM超参数和权重的泄露情况

Result: 首次实现从GPU Tensor Core单元的参数提取；证明GPU电磁辐射在100厘米距离外、透过玻璃障碍物仍会泄露信息

Conclusion: Tensor Core单元同样存在物理侧信道攻击风险，GPU电磁辐射泄露范围远超预期，这对现代ML模型安全构成新的威胁

Abstract: The multi-million dollar investment required for modern machine learning (ML) has made large ML models a prime target for theft. In response, the field of model stealing has emerged. Attacks based on physical side-channel information have shown that DNN model extraction is feasible, even on CUDA Cores in a GPU. For the first time, our work demonstrates parameter extraction on the specialized GPU's Tensor Core units, most commonly used GPU units nowadays due to their superior performance, via near-field physical side-channel attacks. Previous work targeted only the general-purpose CUDA Cores in the GPU, the functional units that have been part of the GPU since its inception. Our method is tailored to the GPU architecture to accurately estimate energy consumption and derive efficient attacks via Correlation Power Analysis (CPA). Furthermore, we provide an exploratory analysis of hyperparameter and weight leakage from LLMs in far field and demonstrate that the GPU's electromagnetic radiation leaks even 100\,cm away through a glass obstacle.

</details>


### [12] [Contextualized Privacy Defense for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02983)
*Yule Wen,Yanzhe Zhang,Jianxun Lian,Xiaoyuan Yi,Xing Xie,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CDI是一种新的隐私防御范式，通过指令模型在智能体执行过程中生成上下文感知的隐私指导，结合强化学习优化，在隐私保护(94.2%)和有用性(80.6%)之间取得更好平衡


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体隐私防御方法存在局限性：大多数采用静态或被动的防御方式（如提示和防护），无法支持多步骤智能体执行中的上下文感知、主动隐私决策

Method: 提出上下文防御指导(CDI)范式：1) 指令模型在智能体执行过程中生成步骤特定、上下文感知的隐私指导；2) 采用经验驱动的优化框架，通过强化学习训练指令模型，将包含隐私违规的失败轨迹转化为学习环境；3) 在统一仿真框架中形式化基线防御和CDI作为不同干预点

Result: CDI在隐私保护(94.2%)和有用性(80.6%)之间取得最佳平衡，优于基线方法，在对抗条件下表现出更好的鲁棒性和泛化能力

Conclusion: CDI通过主动、上下文感知的隐私指导范式，结合强化学习优化，为LLM智能体隐私保护提供了更有效、更灵活的解决方案，能够更好地平衡隐私保护与有用性需求

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly act on users' personal information, yet existing privacy defenses remain limited in both design and adaptability. Most prior approaches rely on static or passive defenses, such as prompting and guarding. These paradigms are insufficient for supporting contextual, proactive privacy decisions in multi-step agent execution. We propose Contextualized Defense Instructing (CDI), a new privacy defense paradigm in which an instructor model generates step-specific, context-aware privacy guidance during execution, proactively shaping actions rather than merely constraining or vetoing them. Crucially, CDI is paired with an experience-driven optimization framework that trains the instructor via reinforcement learning (RL), where we convert failure trajectories with privacy violations into learning environments. We formalize baseline defenses and CDI as distinct intervention points in a canonical agent loop, and compare their privacy-helpfulness trade-offs within a unified simulation framework. Results show that our CDI consistently achieves a better balance between privacy preservation (94.2%) and helpfulness (80.6%) than baselines, with superior robustness to adversarial conditions and generalization.

</details>


### [13] [RAIN: Secure and Robust Aggregation under Shuffle Model of Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03108)
*Yuhang Li,Yajie Wang,Xiangyun Tang,Peng Jiang,Yu-an Tan,Liehuang Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RAIN框架在Shuffle-DP下统一实现隐私保护、鲁棒性和可验证性，通过符号空间聚合抵抗投毒攻击，采用秘密共享协议保护隐私，提供实时完整性验证，显著降低通信成本


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于Shuffle-DP的隐私保护学习系统面临两个主要挑战：1) 本地扰动和全局匿名化会扭曲梯度几何并移除身份关联，使系统易受对抗性投毒攻击；2) 作为第三方的混洗器可能被攻陷，破坏对恶意敌手的安全性

Method: RAIN采用符号空间聚合在噪声和匿名化下鲁棒地测量更新一致性并限制恶意影响。设计了两种新颖的秘密共享协议：1) 直接在加法份额上操作的混洗协议；2) 聚合协议，两者都保持Shuffle-DP的紧隐私保证。每轮聚合结果都经过验证以确保正确聚合并检测选择性丢弃

Result: 在全面基准测试中，RAIN在保持Shuffle-DP强隐私保证的同时，对投毒攻击保持鲁棒性，准确性和收敛性仅有可忽略的下降。提供实时完整性验证和完整的篡改检测，通信成本降低高达90倍，聚合速度提升10倍

Conclusion: RAIN成功解决了Shuffle-DP下隐私保护、鲁棒性和可验证性的统一问题，通过符号空间聚合和秘密共享协议实现了恶意安全性，为隐私保护学习提供了高效实用的解决方案

Abstract: Secure aggregation is a foundational building block of privacy-preserving learning, yet achieving robustness under adversarial behavior remains challenging. Modern systems increasingly adopt the shuffle model of differential privacy (Shuffle-DP) to locally perturb client updates and globally anonymize them via shuffling for enhanced privacy protection. However, these perturbations and anonymization distort gradient geometry and remove identity linkage, leaving systems vulnerable to adversarial poisoning attacks. Moreover, the shuffler, typically a third party, can be compromised, undermining security against malicious adversaries. To address these challenges, we present Robust Aggregation in Noise (RAIN), a unified framework that reconciles privacy, robustness, and verifiability under Shuffle-DP. At its core, RAIN adopts sign-space aggregation to robustly measure update consistency and limit malicious influence under noise and anonymization. Specifically, we design two novel secret-shared protocols for shuffling and aggregation that operate directly on additive shares and preserve Shuffle-DP's tight privacy guarantee. In each round, the aggregated result is verified to ensure correct aggregation and detect any selective dropping, achieving malicious security with minimal overhead. Extensive experiments across comprehensive benchmarks show that RAIN maintains strong privacy guarantees under Shuffle-DP and remains robust to poisoning attacks with negligible degradation in accuracy and convergence. It further provides real-time integrity verification with complete tampering detection, while achieving up to 90x lower communication cost and 10x faster aggregation compared with prior work.

</details>

### [14] [Logics and Type Theory: essays dedicated to Stefano Berardi on the occasion of his 1000000th birthday](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02912)
*Thorsten Altenkirch,Franco Barbanera,Ferruccio Damiani,Ugo de'Liguoro*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 关于证明论与类型论研究领域的论文集，特别致敬Stefano Berardi在该领域的贡献


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 收集证明论与类型论领域的研究成果，展示该研究领域的成就与前景，特别向Stefano Berardi在该领域的贡献致敬

Method: 通过编辑论文集的方式，收录该领域活跃研究者的论文，特别是与Stefano Berardi有过合作的研究者

Result: 形成了一部展示证明论与类型论研究进展的论文集，涵盖构造逻辑、依赖类型、循环证明等主题

Conclusion: 该论文集反映了证明论与类型论领域的研究活力，展示了Stefano Berardi及其合作者对该领域的持续贡献

Abstract: Proof Theory and Type Theory are two branches of mathematical logic and theoretical computer science that explore the structure of mathematical proofs and the foundations of computation. Both are crucial for understanding formal systems, programming languages, and constructive mathematics. Stefano Berardi is an influential researcher in these fields, particularly known for his work on constructive logic, dependent types, and more recently, cyclic proofs. These proceedings intend to collect papers by researchers who are active in the field and often have been coauthors of Stefano, aimed to illustrate the achievements and the perspectives of this research area.

</details>


### [15] [Bidirectional Interpolation for the Lambda-Calculus -- Revisiting and Formalising Craig-Čubrić Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03083)
*Meven Lennon Bertrand,Alexis Saurin*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文基于双向类型化原则，为Čubrić的证明相关插值定理提供了新证明，并在Rocq中进行了形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Craig插值定理在数理逻辑和计算机科学中有广泛应用。虽然已有Maehara和Prawitz的证明论方法，以及Čubrić在简单类型lambda演算中的证明相关版本，但需要基于现代类型理论原则的新证明方法。

Method: 采用双向类型化原则，构建Čubrić证明相关插值定理的新证明，并在Rocq证明辅助系统中进行形式化验证。

Result: 成功基于双向类型化原则给出了Čubrić证明相关插值定理的新证明，并在Rocq中实现了形式化验证。

Conclusion: 双向类型化原则为证明相关插值定理提供了有效的新证明框架，Rocq中的形式化验证确保了证明的正确性，为相关研究提供了可靠基础。

Abstract: Craig's Interpolation theorem has a wide range of applications, from mathematical logic to computer science. Proof-theoretic techniques for establishing interpolation usually follow a method first introduced by Maehara for the Sequent Calculus and then adapted by Prawitz to Natural Deduction. The result can be strengthened to a proof-relevant version, taking proof terms into account: this was first established by Čubrić in the simply-typed lambda-calculus with sums and more recently in linear, classical and intuitionistic sequent calculi. We give a new proof of Čubrić's proof-relevant interpolation theorem by building on principles of bidirectional typing, and formalise it in Rocq.

</details>


### [16] [Yeo's Theorem for Locally Colored Graphs: the Path to Sequentialization in Linear Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03262)
*Rémi Di Guardia,Olivier Laurent,Lorenzo Tortora de Falco,Lionel Vaux Auclair*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 本文重新审视线性逻辑证明网中与Danos-Regnier正确性准则相关的顺序化证明，通过基于半边着色的Yeo定理推广，以抽象方式从证明网中提取顺序信息而不改变其图结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究线性逻辑证明网中顺序化证明的改进方法，旨在在不修改证明网图结构的前提下，以更抽象和模块化的方式从证明网恢复sequent演算推导。

Method: 采用基于半边着色的广义Yeo定理，通过定义专门的着色方案，利用"尖点最小化"关键引理，构造具有更少尖点的循环，从而确保分裂顶点的存在。

Result: 获得了多种从证明网归纳恢复sequent演算推导的方法，可模块化地选择分裂顶点（如par-顶点、终端顶点、非公理顶点等），该方法适用于含mix规则的证明网以及无单位元的乘加线性逻辑证明网。

Conclusion: 提出的方法以更直接的方式推导出多个图论结果，包括已知与Yeo定理等价的结果，相比文献中需要复杂编码的方法，本方法仅通过定义专门的着色而不改变底层图结构即可实现。

Abstract: We revisit sequentialization proofs associated with the Danos-Regnier correctness criterion in the theory of proof nets of linear logic. Our approach relies on a generalization of Yeo's theorem for graphs, based on colorings of half-edges. This happens to be the appropriate level of abstraction to extract sequentiality information from a proof net without modifying its graph structure. We thus obtain different ways of recovering a sequent calculus derivation from a proof net inductively, by relying on a splitting vertex, which we can impose to be a par-vertex, or a terminal vertex, or a non-axiom vertex, etc., in a modular way. This approach applies in presence of the mix-rules as well as for proof nets of unit-free multiplicative-additive linear logic (through an appropriate further generalization of Yeo's theorem).
  The proof of our Yeo-style theorem relies on a key lemma that we call cusp minimization. Given a coloring of half-edges, a cusp in a path is a vertex whose adjacent half-edges in the path have the same color. And, given a cycle with at least one cusp and subject to suitable hypotheses, cusp minimization constructs a cycle with strictly less cusps. In the absence of cusp-free cycles, cusp minimization is then enough to ensure the existence of a splitting vertex, i.e. a vertex that is a cusp of any cycle it belongs to. Our theorem subsumes several graph-theoretical results, including some known to be equivalent to Yeo's theorem. The novelty is that they can be derived in a straightforward way, just by defining a dedicated coloring, again without any modification of the underlying graph structure (vertices and edges) -- similar results from the literature required more involved encodings.

</details>

### [17] [Handling Exceptions and Effects with Automatic Resource Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02260)
*Ethan Chu,Yiyang Guo,Jan Hoffmann*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 首个支持异常/效应处理程序中非局部控制转移的自动资源边界分析系统，扩展了基于类型的自动摊销资源分析(AARA)，在Standard ML中实现并验证了低开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自动资源边界分析技术虽然对大多数程序和语言特性有效，但无法处理异常或效应处理程序所需的非局部控制转移，这限制了分析能力。

Method: 扩展基于类型的自动摊销资源分析(AARA)，为包含列表和线性势函数的简单函数式语言设计新的类型系统，支持异常和效应处理程序中的非局部控制转移。

Result: 实现了首个支持异常/效应处理程序非局部控制转移的自动资源边界分析，能够分析现有方法无法处理的程序，且支持异常和效应处理的效率开销较低。

Conclusion: 成功扩展了AARA以支持异常和效应处理程序，通过新颖的语法类型健全性定理验证了边界正确性，为复杂控制流程序的资源分析提供了可行方案。

Abstract: There exist many techniques for automatically deriving parametric resource (or cost) bounds by analyzing the source code of a program. These techniques work effectively for a large class of programs and language features. However, non-local transfer of control as needed for exception or effect handlers has remained a challenge.
  This paper presents the first automatic resource bound analysis that supports non-local control transfer between exceptions or effects and their handlers. The analysis is an extension of type-based automatic amortized resource analysis (AARA), which automates the potential method of amortized analysis. It is presented for a simple functional language with lists and linear potential functions. However, the ideas are directly applicable to richer settings and implemented for Standard ML and polynomial potential functions.
  Apart from the new type system for exceptions and effects, a main contribution is a novel syntactic type-soundness theorem that establishes the correctness of the derived bounds with respect to a stack-based abstract machine. An experimental evaluation shows that the new analysis is capable of analyzing programs that cannot be analyzed by existing methods and that the efficiency overhead of supporting exception and effect handlers is low.

</details>


### [18] [Highly Incremental: A Simple Programmatic Approach for Many Objectives (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02405)
*Philipp Schröer,Joost-Pieter Katoen*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的程序化方法，通过添加奖励语句和程序变换，利用单调函数处理累积奖励的增量差异，从而统一分析概率程序中的多种目标（高阶矩、阈值概率、预算超额期望、矩生成函数等）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 概率程序分析中需要处理多种不同的目标（如高阶矩、阈值概率、预算超额期望、矩生成函数等），传统方法需要为每种目标开发专门的推理技术。本文旨在提出一种统一的程序化方法，能够用一个框架处理所有这些目标，简化分析过程。

Method: 1. 在概率程序语言中添加奖励语句；2. 定义程序变换，将单调函数应用于程序的累积奖励；3. 关键思想是利用奖励的增量差异；4. 通过这种变换，将各种目标（高阶矩、阈值概率、预算超额期望、矩生成函数）统一表达；5. 所有目标都可以使用现有的概率wp推理方法进行分析；6. 使用Caesar演绎验证器实现自动化验证。

Result: 成功开发了一种统一的程序变换方法，能够表达和分析概率程序中的多种目标。通过Caesar验证器实现了自动化验证，并在一些示例上应用了该变换，证明了方法的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一种简单而优雅的统一方法，通过添加奖励语句和程序变换，利用单调函数处理累积奖励的增量差异，使得概率程序中的多种目标（高阶矩、阈值概率、预算超额期望、矩生成函数）都可以用单一的现有概率wp推理方法进行分析，实现了分析框架的统一化和简化。

Abstract: We present a one-fits-all programmatic approach to reason about a plethora of objectives on probabilistic programs. The first ingredient is to add a reward-statement to the language. We then define a program transformation applying a monotone function to the cumulative reward of the program. The key idea is that this transformation uses incremental differences in the reward. This simple, elegant approach enables to express e.g., higher moments, threshold probabilities of rewards, the expected excess over a budget, and moment-generating functions. All these objectives can now be analyzed using a single existing approach: probabilistic wp-reasoning. We automated verification using the Caesar deductive verifier and report on the application of the transformation to some examples.

</details>


### [19] [Efficient Dynamic Algorithms to Predict Short Races](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03141)
*Minjian Zhang,Mahesh Viswanathan*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 提出并研究了在观测轨迹中检测短竞争条件的问题，针对特定竞争类型R，在窗口大小w内高效检测竞争事件对(e₁, e₂)，开发了监控框架并实例化为happens-before和sync-preserving竞争检测算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有竞争检测算法在处理长轨迹时面临资源消耗大的问题，特别是内存使用随轨迹长度对数增长。短竞争检测能够更高效地利用有限资源，在相同预算下检测更多竞争，平衡资源使用和预测能力。

Method: 提出了短竞争检测的监控框架，针对happens-before竞争开发了与FastTrack时间复杂度相同但空间复杂度为log w而非log |σ|的算法；针对sync-preserving竞争开发了比SyncP更快、内存消耗更少的算法。

Result: 实验验证了短竞争检测算法的有效性：运行更高效，内存使用更少，在相同预算下检测到显著更多的竞争，在资源使用和预测能力之间提供了合理平衡。

Conclusion: 短竞争检测框架为竞争预测提供了实用解决方案，通过限制检测窗口大小实现了资源效率与检测能力的良好权衡，为实际系统中的竞争检测提供了更可行的选择。

Abstract: We introduce and study the problem of detecting short races in an observed trace. Specifically, for a race type $R$, given a trace $σ$ and window size $w$, the task is to determine whether there exists an $R$-race $(e_1, e_2)$ in $σ$ such that the subtrace starting with $e_1$ and ending with $e_2$ contains at most $w$ events. We present a monitoring framework for short-race prediction and instantiate the framework for happens-before and sync-preserving races, yielding efficient detection algorithms. Our happens-before algorithm runs in the same time as FastTrack but uses space that scales with $\log w$ as opposed to $\log |σ|$. For sync-preserving races, our algorithm runs faster and consumes significantly less space than SyncP. Our experiments validate the effectiveness of these short-race detection algorithms: they run more efficiently, use less memory, and detect significantly more races under the same budget, offering a reasonable balance between resource usage and predictive power.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

### [20] [Strategic Shaping of Human Prosociality: A Latent-State POMDP Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02379)
*Zahra Zahedi,Xinyue Hu,Shashank Mehrotra,Mark Steyvers,Kumar Akash*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个决策理论框架，让机器人能够在重复交互中策略性地塑造推断的人类亲社会状态


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人类-机器人协作中，人类的内在亲社会状态会影响合作行为，但机器人通常无法直接观测这种状态。需要让机器人能够主动影响和塑造人类的亲社会状态，以促进长期合作

Method: 将人类亲社会性建模为随时间演化的潜在状态，构建具有有限观测的潜在状态POMDP，使用期望最大化学习状态转移和观测动态，基于信念的策略平衡任务和社会目标

Result: 通过用户研究数据评估，学习到的策略在团队绩效和增加观察到的合作行为方面均优于基线策略

Conclusion: 该框架使机器人能够有效推断并策略性地影响人类的亲社会状态，促进长期合作结果，为社交智能机器人设计提供了新方法

Abstract: We propose a decision-theoretic framework in which a robot strategically can shape inferred human's prosocial state during repeated interactions. Modeling the human's prosociality as a latent state that evolves over time, the robot learns to infer and influence this state through its own actions, including helping and signaling. We formalize this as a latent-state POMDP with limited observations and learn the transition and observation dynamics using expectation maximization. The resulting belief-based policy balances task and social objectives, selecting actions that maximize long-term cooperative outcomes. We evaluate the model using data from user studies and show that the learned policy outperforms baseline strategies in both team performance and increasing observed human cooperative behavior.

</details>


### [21] [Pulli Kolam: A Traditional South Indian Craft Practice for Representing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02343)
*Shri Harini Ramesh,Fateme Rajabiyazdi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 将南印度传统工艺Pulli Kolam作为物理数据表示媒介，通过五种映射策略在不破坏传统实践的前提下嵌入数据表示


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索如何将数据表示嵌入传统文化实践中，特别是利用Pulli Kolam这种具有文化意义和具身实践的传统工艺，实现数据物理表示与日常实践的融合

Method: 识别Pulli Kolam中的五种映射策略：点、图案、填充、线条和颜色，这些策略可用于物理数据表示而不破坏传统实践；通过日常幸福感追踪的场景演示如何将数据表示嵌入日常工艺实践

Result: 成功展示了Pulli Kolam作为数据物理表示媒介的可行性，提出了五种具体的映射策略，并通过日常幸福感追踪场景验证了数据表示可以无缝融入传统工艺实践

Conclusion: Pulli Kolam可以作为有效的物理数据表示媒介，同时保持其具身和仪式特性；提出了超越其短暂形式的材料适应性扩展方案，为传统文化实践与现代数据表示的结合提供了新途径

Abstract: This paper introduces Pulli Kolam, a traditional South Indian craft, as a medium for physical data representation. Grounded in its cultural meaning and embodied practice, Pulli Kolam follows structured geometric rules while allowing creative variation. We identify five mapping strategies within Kolam (dots, patterns, fills, lines, and color) that can be used for representing data physically. without disrupting traditional practice. Through an illustrative scenario of daily well-being tracking, we demonstrate how data representation can be embedded within routine craft practice. We conclude by outlining potential material adaptations that extend Kolam beyond its ephemeral form while maintaining its embodied and ritual qualities.

</details>


### [22] [PlayWrite: A Multimodal System for AI Supported Narrative Co-Authoring Through Play in XR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02366)
*Esen K. Tütüncü,Qian Zhou,Frederik Brudy,George Fitzmaurice,Fraser Anderson*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: PlayWrite是一个混合现实故事创作系统，用户通过直接操纵虚拟角色和道具来创作故事，AI将动作解释为可重排的"故事弹珠"，最终生成完整叙事。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于文本提示的AI写作工具难以支持故事创作的空间性和交互性本质，无法满足通过直接操作和游戏来产生创意的需求。

Method: 开发混合现实系统，用户直接操纵虚拟角色和道具；采用多智能体AI管道将动作解释为结构化叙事节拍（Intent Frames），可视化为时间线上的可重排"故事弹珠"；使用大语言模型将用户组装的序列转换为最终叙事。

Result: 用户研究（N=13）显示，PlayWrite促进了高度即兴和游戏化的创作过程，用户将AI视为协作伙伴，利用其意外响应激发新想法并克服创意障碍。

Conclusion: PlayWrite展示了一种超越文本的协同创作系统方法，将直接操作和游戏作为核心交互模式，为AI辅助创作提供了新范式。

Abstract: Current AI writing tools, which rely on text prompts, poorly support the spatial and interactive nature of storytelling where ideas emerge from direct manipulation and play. We present PlayWrite, a mixed-reality system where users author stories by directly manipulating virtual characters and props. A multi-agent AI pipeline interprets these actions into Intent Frames -structured narrative beats visualized as rearrangeable story marbles on a timeline. A large language model then transforms the user's assembled sequence into a final narrative. A user study (N=13) with writers from varying domains found that PlayWrite fosters a highly improvisational and playful process. Users treated the AI as a collaborative partner, using its unexpected responses to spark new ideas and overcome creative blocks. PlayWrite demonstrates an approach for co-creative systems that move beyond text to embrace direct manipulation and play as core interaction modalities.

</details>


### [23] [Learning to Pay Attention: Unsupervised Modeling of Attentive and Inattentive Respondents in Survey Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02427)
*Ilias Triantafyllopoulos,Panos Ipeirotis*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出无标签框架检测调查问卷中的不专注受访者，利用自编码器和Chow-Liu树评估回答一致性，发现检测效果主要取决于问卷结构而非模型复杂度


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统注意力检查方法成本高、反应式且不一致，需要一种统一的无标签框架来检测行为和社会科学调查中的不专注受访者，这些受访者提供随机或低努力的回答

Method: 提出统一的无标签框架，通过互补的无监督视角评估回答一致性：几何重建（自编码器）和概率依赖建模（Chow-Liu树）。引入"百分位数损失"目标以提高自编码器对异常的鲁棒性

Result: 在九个异质真实世界数据集上，发现检测效果主要受调查结构驱动而非模型复杂度：具有连贯、重叠项目组的工具展现出强协方差模式，即使线性模型也能可靠区分专注和不专注受访者

Conclusion: 揭示了关键的"心理测量-ML对齐"：最大化测量可靠性的设计原则（如内部一致性）也最大化算法可检测性。该框架为调查平台提供了可扩展、领域无关的诊断工具，将数据质量直接与工具设计联系起来

Abstract: The integrity of behavioral and social-science surveys depends on detecting inattentive respondents who provide random or low-effort answers. Traditional safeguards, such as attention checks, are often costly, reactive, and inconsistent. We propose a unified, label-free framework for inattentiveness detection that scores response coherence using complementary unsupervised views: geometric reconstruction (Autoencoders) and probabilistic dependency modeling (Chow-Liu trees). While we introduce a "Percentile Loss" objective to improve Autoencoder robustness against anomalies, our primary contribution is identifying the structural conditions that enable unsupervised quality control. Across nine heterogeneous real-world datasets, we find that detection effectiveness is driven less by model complexity than by survey structure: instruments with coherent, overlapping item batteries exhibit strong covariance patterns that allow even linear models to reliably separate attentive from inattentive respondents. This reveals a critical ``Psychometric-ML Alignment'': the same design principles that maximize measurement reliability (e.g., internal consistency) also maximize algorithmic detectability. The framework provides survey platforms with a scalable, domain-agnostic diagnostic tool that links data quality directly to instrument design, enabling auditing without additional respondent burden.

</details>


### [24] [Improving Low-Vision Chart Accessibility via On-Cursor Visual Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02498)
*Yotam Sechayk,Hennes Rave,Max Rädler,Mark Colley,Zhongyi Zhou,Ariel Shamir,Takeo Igarashi*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该论文提出两种指针交互方法（动态上下文和迷你地图）来改善低视力人群对图表的访问，通过提供关键上下文信息（坐标轴、图例、网格线、概览）来解决他们在全局上下文理解方面的困难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图表对低视力人群（LVI）的可访问性不足。阅读图表需要在全局上下文中查看数据点，这对依赖放大或存在部分视野缺陷的LVI来说很困难。当前工具缺乏对关键上下文信息的有效访问支持。

Method: 1）通过5名LVI的探索性研究识别出四个基本上下文元素：坐标轴、图例、网格线和概览；2）提出两种指针交互方法：动态上下文（新颖的焦点+上下文交互）和迷你地图（为LVI调整的概览+细节原则）；3）在N=22名LVI中进行研究，比较两种方法并评估其与现有工具的集成。

Result: 动态上下文在可访问性、可用性和减少认知负担方面有显著积极影响，但增加了视觉负荷。迷你地图增强了空间理解，但在此任务中较少被偏好。两种方法各有优劣，需要权衡视觉负荷。

Conclusion: 为支持LVI的图表访问，需要提供视觉上下文同时平衡视觉负荷。论文提供了设计见解来指导未来系统的开发，强调在动态上下文和迷你地图方法之间根据具体需求进行选择。

Abstract: Despite widespread use, charts remain largely inaccessible for Low-Vision Individuals (LVI). Reading charts requires viewing data points within a global context, which is difficult for LVI who may rely on magnification or experience a partial field of vision. We aim to improve exploration by providing visual access to critical context. To inform this, we conducted a formative study with five LVI. We identified four fundamental contextual elements common across chart types: axes, legend, grid lines, and the overview. We propose two pointer-based interaction methods to provide this context: Dynamic Context, a novel focus+context interaction, and Mini-map, which adapts overview+detail principles for LVI. In a study with N=22 LVI, we compared both methods and evaluated their integration to current tools. Our results show that Dynamic Context had significant positive impact on access, usability, and effort reduction; however, worsened visual load. Mini-map strengthened spatial understanding, but was less preferred for this task. We offer design insights to guide the development of future systems that support LVI with visual context while balancing visual load.

</details>


### [25] [Understanding the Effects of Interaction on Emotional Experiences in VR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02535)
*Zheyuan Kuang,Tinghui Li,Weiwei Jiang,Sven Mayer,Flora Salim,Benjamin Tag,Anusha Withana,Zhanna Sarsenbayeva*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究扩展了已验证的VR情感诱发数据集，通过添加新的高唤醒高效价场景并引入交互元素，探究交互对情感反应的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VR情感诱发研究主要关注情感反应的强度，而忽略了交互如何影响这些情感体验。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探究交互在VR情感诱发中的作用。

Method: 1. 添加新的高唤醒高效价场景，并通过被试内研究(N=24)验证其有效性；2. 为每个场景创建交互和非交互版本；3. 采用多模态方法评估交互影响，结合主观评分和生理信号来捕捉有意识和无意识的情感反应；4. 通过评估研究(N=84)分析交互对情感的影响。

Result: 交互不仅放大了情感反应，还在不同情境下调节情感：在负面场景中支持应对机制，在正面场景中增强愉悦感。这表明交互能够根据场景特点调制情感体验。

Conclusion: 场景定制的交互在不同应用中具有重要潜力，特别是在需要调节情感而不仅仅是诱发情感的场景中。交互能够根据情境特点调制情感反应，为情感调节应用提供了新方向。

Abstract: Virtual reality has been effectively used for eliciting emotions, yet most research focuses on the intensity of affective responses rather than on how interaction influences those experiences. To address this gap, we advance a validated VR emotion-elicitation dataset through two key extensions. First, we add a new high-arousal, high-valence scene and validate its effectiveness in a within-subject study (N=24). Second, we incorporate interactive elements into each scene, creating both interactive and non-interactive versions to examine the impact of interaction on emotional responses. We evaluate interaction through a multimodal approach combining subjective ratings and physiological signals to capture both conscious and unconscious affective responses. Our evaluation study (N=84) shows that interaction not only amplifies emotions but modulates them in context, supporting coping in negative scenes and enhancing enjoyment in positive scenes. These findings highlight the potential of scene-tailored interaction for different applications, where regulating emotions is as important as eliciting them.

</details>


### [26] [Orality: A Semantic Canvas for Externalizing and Clarifying Thoughts with Speech](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02544)
*Wengxi Li,Jingze Tian,Can Liu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: Orality是一个支持通过言语化思考的系统，它从口语内容中提取关键信息，通过LLM进行语义分析形成交互式节点链接图，帮助用户澄清和组织思维。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人们通过大声说话来外化思想以帮助澄清和组织它们，但语音转文字产生的转录稿由于不流畅、重复和潜在的无组织性而难以阅读和理解。

Method: Orality从口语内容中提取关键信息，通过LLM进行语义分析，在交互式画布中形成节点链接图。用户可以操作节点集群，通过语音指令重新提取和组织内容，系统还提供AI生成的启发式问题和逻辑冲突检测。

Result: 通过12名参与者的实验室研究，比较Orality与ChatGPT的语音交互，发现Orality能更好地支持用户澄清和发展他们的思想，并识别了图形和对话式思维澄清工具的不同功能特性。

Conclusion: Orality通过图形化表示和交互式操作，比传统的语音转录或对话式AI能更有效地支持言语化思考过程，为思维澄清工具的设计提供了重要启示。

Abstract: People speak aloud to externalize thoughts as one way to help clarify and organize them. Although Speech-to-text can capture these thoughts, transcripts can be difficult to read and make sense due to disfluencies, repetitions and potential disorganization. To support thinking through verbalization, we introduce Orality, which extracts key information from spoken content, performs semantic analysis through LLMs to form a node-link diagram in an interactive canvas. Instead of reading and working with transcripts, users could manipulate clusters of nodes and give verbal instructions to re-extract and organize the content in other ways. It also provides AI-generated inspirational questions and detection of logical conflicts. We conducted a lab study with twelve participants comparing Orality against speech interaction with ChatGPT. We found that Orality can better support users in clarifying and developing their thoughts. The findings also identified the affordances of both graphical and conversational thought clarification tools and derived design implications.

</details>


### [27] ['Show It, Don't Just Say It': The Complementary Effects of Instruction Multimodality for Software Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02567)
*Emran Poh,Yueyue Hou,Tianyi Zhang,Jiannan Li*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究通过观察10对师生在图形设计软件教学中的交互，分析了三种通信渠道（语音、视觉标注、远程屏幕控制）在自适应辅导系统设计中的应用，发现了精度与学习者自主性之间的权衡关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为自适应软件学习辅导系统的设计提供依据，研究如何确定合适的教学模态，特别是当系统只能使用有限的通信渠道（类似AI导师可能具备的功能）时。

Method: 观察性研究：10对有经验的图形设计软件用户（教师）教新手两个新软件功能的多步骤程序。教学仅限于三种通信渠道：语音、视觉标注和远程屏幕控制，以模拟AI导师可能的模态。

Result: 标注通过空间精度补充语音，远程控制通过空间和时间精度补充语音，但两者都会侵犯学习者的自主性。教师自适应选择模态以平衡教学进度需求与学生的认知参与度和数字领地所有权感。

Conclusion: 研究结果支持邻近性原则和学习者自主性的价值，同时提出了精度-自主性权衡和数字领地性作为自适应软件指导的新设计约束。

Abstract: Designing adaptive tutoring systems for software learning presents challenges in determining appropriate instructional modalities. To inform the design of such systems, we conducted an observational study of ten human teacher-student pairs (N=10), where experienced design software users taught novices two new graphic design software features through multi-step procedures. These lessons were limited to three communication channels (speech, visual annotations, and remote screen control) to mimic possible AI tutor modalities. We found that annotations complement speech with spatial precision and remote control complements it with spatial and temporal precision, but both cause intrusion to learner agency. Teachers adaptively select modalities to balance the need for instruction progress with students' cognitive engagement and sense of digital territory ownership. Our results provide further support to the contiguity principles and the value of agency in learning, while suggesting precision-agency trade-off and digital territoriality as new design constraints for adaptive software guidance.

</details>


### [28] [An LLM-Assisted Toolkit for Inspectable Multimodal Emotion Data Annotation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02569)
*Zheyuan Kuang,Weiwei Jiang,Nicholas Koemel,Matthew Ahmadi,Emmanuel Stamatakis,Benjamin Tag,Anusha Withana,Zhanna Sarsenbayeva*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出一个LLM辅助的多模态情感数据标注工具包，通过事件中心的可检查工作流程支持标注


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态情感识别越来越依赖细粒度、基于证据的标注，但当线索在跨模态间动态且不对齐时，检查和标签构建难以扩展

Method: 开发LLM辅助工具包：1)预处理和对齐异构记录；2)在交互式共享时间线上可视化所有模态；3)将结构化信号渲染为视频轨道进行跨模态一致性检查；4)检测候选事件并打包同步关键帧和时间窗口作为事件包；5)集成LLM与模态特定工具和提示模板来起草结构化标注供分析师验证和编辑

Result: 在具有代表性的多模态VR情感记录上演示了该工作流程

Conclusion: 该工具包通过事件中心的可检查工作流程支持多模态情感数据标注，解决了动态、不对齐跨模态线索下的标注可扩展性问题

Abstract: Multimodal Emotion Recognition (MER) increasingly depends on fine grained, evidence grounded annotations, yet inspection and label construction are hard to scale when cues are dynamic and misaligned across modalities. We present an LLM-assisted toolkit that supports multimodal emotion data annotation through an inspectable, event centered workflow. The toolkit preprocesses and aligns heterogeneous recordings, visualizes all modalities on an interactive shared timeline, and renders structured signals as video tracks for cross modal consistency checks. It then detects candidate events and packages synchronized keyframes and time windows as event packets with traceable pointers to the source data. Finally, the toolkit integrates an LLM with modality specific tools and prompt templates to draft structured annotations for analyst verification and editing. We demonstrate the workflow on multimodal VR emotion recordings with representative examples.

</details>


### [29] [Behavior-Aware Anthropometric Scene Generation for Human-Usable 3D Layouts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02662)
*Semin Jin,Donghyuk Kim,Jeongmin Ryu,Kyung Hoon Hyun*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出行为感知人体测量场景生成框架，通过视觉语言模型分析对象-行为关系，将空间需求转化为适应特定用户人体测量数据的参数化布局约束，改善室内场景的功能性和可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D场景生成方法过于关注视觉和语义合理性，而忽视了场景是否支持人类舒适地行走、坐下或操作物体等行为需求，需要将人类行为因素纳入场景生成过程。

Method: 使用视觉语言模型分析对象与行为的关系，将空间需求转化为参数化布局约束，并适应特定用户的人体测量数据，实现行为感知的场景生成。

Result: 与最先进模型相比，在几何指标和用户感知研究中表现更优；在个体和群体的人体尺度研究中，任务完成时间、轨迹效率和人物操作空间均有改善。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一个将基于VLM的交互推理与人体测量约束相结合的框架，通过技术指标和真实尺度的人类可用性研究验证了其有效性，为功能性室内场景生成提供了新方法。

Abstract: Well-designed indoor scenes should prioritize how people can act within a space rather than merely what objects to place. However, existing 3D scene generation methods emphasize visual and semantic plausibility, while insufficiently addressing whether people can comfortably walk, sit, or manipulate objects. To bridge this gap, we present a Behavior-Aware Anthropometric Scene Generation framework. Our approach leverages vision-language models (VLMs) to analyze object-behavior relationships, translating spatial requirements into parametric layout constraints adapted to user-specific anthropometric data. We conducted comparative studies with state-of-the-art models using geometric metrics and a user perception study (N=16). We further conducted in-depth human-scale studies (individuals, N=20; groups, N=18). The results showed improvements in task completion time, trajectory efficiency, and human-object manipulation space. This study contributes a framework that bridges VLM-based interaction reasoning with anthropometric constraints, validated through both technical metrics and real-scale human usability studies.

</details>


### [30] ["It's Messy...But I Feel Balanced": Unpacking Flexible Workers' Rhythm-Making Practices Using an Asset-Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02841)
*Tse Pei Ng,Daniel Campos-Muniz,Yiyang He,Ker Wey Aw,Jung-Joo Lee,Janghee Cho*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究通过资产视角重新审视灵活工作，将其视为持续的节奏创造实践而非静态福利，揭示了工作者如何利用时空、关系和内在资产来维持工作与照护责任的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管灵活工作被视为实现工作生活平衡的手段，但现有HCI研究主要从生产力和组织视角考察灵活性，较少关注其如何与工作者的个人和家庭责任相交织。本研究旨在填补这一空白，探索工作者如何通过灵活安排来协调有偿工作和照护责任。

Method: 在新加坡对20名采用灵活安排来管理工作与照护责任的工作者进行质性研究，采用资产视角分析框架。

Result: 研究发现灵活性不是静态福利，而是持续的节奏创造实践。参与者通过时空资产维持节奏，通过关系和制度动态协商节奏，并通过自我关怀和积极重构等内在资产维持节奏。研究将模糊边界重新定义为资源而非干扰。

Conclusion: 研究为支持灵活工作者日常节奏创造实践的技术设计提供了启示，强调需要超越传统生产力视角，关注工作者在协调多重责任时的节奏维持策略。

Abstract: Flexible work is increasingly pursued as a means of achieving work-life balance, particularly as growing caregiving responsibilities for children and aging family members shape workers' lives. Yet most HCI research has examined flexibility primarily through productivity and organizational perspectives, with less attention to how it intersects with workers' personal and family responsibilities. To address this gap, we conducted a qualitative study with 20 workers in Singapore engaging in flexible arrangements to manage paid work and care responsibilities. Using an asset-based lens, we show that flexibility is not a static benefit but a continual practice of rhythm-making. Participants maintained rhythms by drawing on temporal and spatial assets, negotiated them through relational and institutional dynamics, and sustained them through intrapersonal assets such as self-care and positive reframing. Our study reframes blurred boundaries as resources rather than disruptions and offers design implications for technologies that support flexible workers' everyday rhythm-making practices.

</details>


### [31] [Speech recognition assisted by large language models to command software orally -- Application to an augmented and virtual reality web app for immersive molecular graphics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02901)
*Fabio Cortes Rodriguez,Luciano Abriata*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 开发了用于沉浸式分子图形Web应用的语音用户界面，结合Chrome ASR和GPT-4o-mini函数调用，解决AR/VR环境中手部操作与界面控制的冲突


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在AR/VR沉浸式分子图形应用中，用户需要用手操作分子，无法同时使用传统鼠标键盘GUI进行应用控制，因此需要开发免手操作的语音界面

Method: 1) 评估Chrome Speech API和OpenAI Whisper v3两种ASR系统，最终选择Chrome ASR；2) 测试代码生成和函数调用两种LLM驱动方法，采用GPT-4o-mini函数调用方案；3) 集成Chrome ASR与LLM函数调用模块构建完整VUI系统

Result: 成功开发了稳定可靠的VUI系统，Chrome ASR在科学术语识别上表现更稳定，GPT-4o-mini函数调用方法在安全性、效率和可靠性上优于代码生成方法，系统可通过自然语言控制应用

Conclusion: 集成Chrome ASR与GPT-4o-mini函数调用的VUI系统有效解决了沉浸式分子图形应用中手部操作与界面控制的冲突，提供了自然、安全、高效的控制方式

Abstract: This project successfully developed, evaluated and integrated a Voice User Interface (VUI) into a web application that we are developing for immersive molecular graphics. Said app provides augmented and virtual reality (AR and VR) environments where users manipulate molecules with their hands, but this means the hands can't be used to control the app through a regular mouse- and keyboard-based GUI. The speech-based VUI system developed here alleviates this problem, making it easy to control the app via natural spoken (or typed) commands. To achieve this VUI we evaluated two distinct Automated Speech Recognition (ASR) systems: Chrome's native Speech API and OpenAI's Whisper v3. While Whisper offered broader browser compatibility, its tendency to "hallucinate" with specialized scientific jargon proved very problematic. Consequently, we selected Chrome's ASR for its stability, speed, and reliability. For translating transcribed speech into software commands, we tested two Large Language Model (LLM)-driven approaches: either generating executable code, or calling predefined functions. The function call method, powered by OpenAI's GPT-4o-mini, was ultimately adopted due to its superior safety, efficiency, and reliability over the more complex and error-prone code-generation approach. The resulting VUI is then based on an integration of Chrome's ASR with our LLM-based function-calling module, enabling users to command the application using natural language as shown in a video linked inside this report. We provide links to live examples demonstrating all the intermediate components, and details on how we crafted the LLM's prompt in order to teach it the function calls as well as ways to clean up the transcribed speech and to explain itself while generating function calls. For best demonstration of the final system, we provide a video example.

</details>


### [32] [Design Generative AI for Practitioners: Exploring Interaction Approaches Aligned with Creative Practice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03074)
*Xiaohan Peng,Wendy E. Mackay,Janin Koch*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出三种交互方法（DesignPrompt、FusAIn、DesignTrace）来改善生成式AI与设计师意图的对齐问题，强调对齐是动态协商过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于提示或全图像操作的AI交互方式与设计师的非线性、反思性设计过程不匹配，迫使视觉思考者进行语言推理或事后调整，导致AI输出与设计师意图错位。

Method: 提出三种交互方法：DesignPrompt、FusAIn和DesignTrace，将控制分布在意图、输入和过程三个层面，使设计师能在不同交互阶段引导AI对齐。

Result: 通过这三种方法，设计师能够更有效地将AI输出与设计意图对齐，AI可以根据设计师的工具性和启发性需求以及创意阶段采取主动或被动角色。

Conclusion: AI与设计师的对齐是动态协商过程，需要交互方法支持设计师在不同阶段引导AI，实现更符合设计思维过程的协作。

Abstract: Design is a non-linear, reflective process in which practitioners engage with visual, semantic, and other expressive materials to explore, iterate, and refine ideas. As Generative AI (GenAI) becomes integrated into professional design practice, traditional interaction approaches focusing on prompts or whole-image manipulation can misalign AI output with designers' intent, forcing visual thinkers into verbal reasoning or post-hoc adjustments. We present three interaction approaches from DesignPrompt, FusAIn, and DesignTrace that distribute control across intent, input, and process, enabling designers to guide AI alignment at different stages of interaction. We further argue that alignment is a dynamic negotiation, with AI adopting proactive or reactive roles according to designers' instrumental and inspirational needs and the creative stage.

</details>


### [33] [Surveillance, Spacing, Screaming and Scabbing: How Digital Technology Facilitates Union Busting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03130)
*Frederick Reiber,Nathan Kim,Allison McDonald,Dana Calacci*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文分析了美国工会组织面临的雇主反组织技术策略，通过亚马逊、星巴克和某大学的案例研究，识别出四种技术战术：监控、间隔、尖叫和破坏，并探讨了数字化工作场所中的工人抵抗策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管美国工会支持率高且工人组织意愿增强，但员工在争取集体谈判协议时仍面临重大障碍。关键因素是雇主反组织策略：通过规则变更、报复和干扰来压制工会化。需要深入理解计算技术在反工会组织中的作用，以设计抵抗这些策略的社会技术工具和战略。

Method: 使用公开可用资料，研究了三个高知名度的组织努力案例：亚马逊、星巴克和某大学。通过案例分析方法，识别出四种反复出现的技术战术：监控(surveillance)、间隔(spacing)、尖叫(screaming)和破坏(scabbing)。分析了这些战术在不同情境下的运作方式，强调了它们的数字维度和战略部署。

Result: 识别并分析了四种雇主反工会组织的技术战术：1) 监控：数字监控技术用于监视工人活动；2) 间隔：技术手段创造物理或数字距离以阻碍组织；3) 尖叫：通过数字渠道传播反工会信息；4) 破坏：使用技术破坏团结和组织努力。这些战术在不同案例中表现出相似的模式和数字维度。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了数字化工作场所中雇主反组织策略的技术维度，为组织工作提供了重要启示。指出了未来研究方向，并探讨了工人抵抗的新兴形式，强调了在技术中介的工作环境中发展有效抵抗策略的重要性。

Abstract: Despite high approval ratings for unions and growing worker interest in organizing, employees in the United States still face significant barriers to securing collective bargaining agreements. A key factor is employer counter-organizing: efforts to suppress unionization through rule changes, retaliation, and disruption. Designing sociotechnical tools and strategies to resist these tactics requires a deeper understanding of the role computing technologies play in counter-organizing against unionization. In this paper, we examine three high-profile organizing efforts -- at Amazon, Starbucks, and \university -- using publicly available sources to identify four recurring technological tactics: surveillance, spacing, screaming and scabbing. We analyze how these tactics operate across contexts, highlighting their digital dimensions and strategic deployment. We conclude with implications for organizing in digitally-mediated workplaces, directions for future research, and emergent forms of worker resistance.

</details>


### [34] [Deception by Design: A Temporal Dark Patterns Audit of McDonald's Self-Ordering Kiosk Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03218)
*Aditya Kumar Purohit,Yuwei Liu,Manon Berney,Hendrik Heuer,Adrian Holzer*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 对麦当劳德国自助点餐机进行结构化审计，发现其界面设计中存在多种暗黑模式，这些模式在交互流程中累积并可能被系统的线性任务结构和物理环境放大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自助点餐机在快餐店广泛部署，虽然提高了效率和平均订单价值，但也为操纵性界面设计（暗黑模式）创造了机会。需要对这些混合物理-数字消费者界面进行更严格的审查。

Method: 使用时间性暗黑模式分析框架，通过基于场景的走查模拟时间紧迫的用户，重构并分析了12个界面步骤，涵盖页面内、页面间和系统三个层次。

Result: 识别出通过中层模式（如添加步骤、虚假层次、不良默认值、隐藏信息、压力销售）和底层模式（如视觉突出、确认羞辱、稀缺框架、前馈模糊、情感感官操纵、分区定价）实现的高层策略。这些模式在交互流程中累积，并可能被点餐机的线性任务结构和物理环境放大。

Conclusion: 混合物理-数字消费者界面需要在新兴的暗黑模式监管讨论中得到更密切的关注，因为这些界面中的暗黑模式可能通过其线性结构和物理环境被放大。

Abstract: Self-ordering kiosks (SOKs) are widely deployed in fast food restaurants, transforming food ordering into digitally mediated, self-navigated interactions. While these systems enhance efficiency and average order value, they also create opportunities for manipulative interface design practices known as dark patterns. This paper presents a structured audit of the McDonald's self-ordering kiosk in Germany using the Temporal Analysis of Dark Patterns (TADP) framework. Through a scenario-based walkthrough simulating a time-pressured user, we reconstructed and analyzed 12 interface steps across intra-page, inter-page, and system levels. We identify recurring high-level strategies implemented through meso-level patterns such as adding steps, false hierarchy, bad defaults, hiding information, and pressured selling, and low-level patterns including visual prominence, confirmshaming, scarcity framing, feedforward ambiguity, emotional sensory manipulation, and partitioned pricing. Our findings demonstrate how these patterns accumulate across the interaction flow and may be amplified by the kiosk's linear task structure and physical context. These findings suggest that hybrid physical--digital consumer interfaces warrant closer scrutiny within emerging regulatory discussions on dark patterns.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [35] [Length Generalization Bounds for Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02238)
*Andy Yang,Pascal Bergsträßer,Georg Zetzsche,David Chiang,Anthony W. Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文研究了CRASP（与transformer密切相关的语言类）的长度泛化边界可计算性问题，证明了CRASP（即使是两层）不存在可计算的长度泛化边界，但对于正片段CRASP（等价于固定精度transformer）存在可计算边界，并证明了边界的指数复杂性和最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长度泛化是学习算法的关键属性，需要计算长度泛化边界来保证模型在任何长度输入上的正确预测。目前CRASP（与transformer紧密相关的语言类）的长度泛化边界可计算性是一个开放问题，之前仅对单层和受限两层有部分正面结果。

Method: 通过理论分析研究CRASP语言类的计算性质，主要采用可计算性理论和复杂度分析的方法，对比不同CRASP变体的计算特性，特别是研究正片段CRASP与固定精度transformer的等价关系。

Result: 1. 主要结果：CRASP（即使是两层）不存在可计算的长度泛化边界，因此transformer也不存在可计算的长度泛化边界。2. 补充结果：正片段CRASP存在可计算的长度泛化边界，且等价于固定精度transformer。3. 复杂度结果：正CRASP和固定精度transformer的长度复杂度是指数级的，并证明了边界的最优性。

Conclusion: 该论文完全解决了CRASP长度泛化边界的可计算性问题：一般CRASP（包括transformer）不存在可计算的边界，但正片段CRASP（等价于固定精度transformer）存在可计算的指数边界且最优。这为理解transformer类模型的长度泛化能力提供了理论界限。

Abstract: Length generalization is a key property of a learning algorithm that enables it to make correct predictions on inputs of any length, given finite training data. To provide such a guarantee, one needs to be able to compute a length generalization bound, beyond which the model is guaranteed to generalize. This paper concerns the open problem of the computability of such generalization bounds for CRASP, a class of languages which is closely linked to transformers. A positive partial result was recently shown by Chen et al. for CRASP with only one layer and, under some restrictions, also with two layers. We provide complete answers to the above open problem. Our main result is the non-existence of computable length generalization bounds for CRASP (already with two layers) and hence for transformers. To complement this, we provide a computable bound for the positive fragment of CRASP, which we show equivalent to fixed-precision transformers. For both positive CRASP and fixed-precision transformers, we show that the length complexity is exponential, and prove optimality of the bounds.

</details>


### [36] [RxnNano:Training Compact LLMs for Chemical Reaction and Retrosynthesis Prediction via Hierarchical Curriculum Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02215)
*Ran Li,Shimin Di,Haowei LI,Luanshi Bu,Jiachuan Wang,Wangze Ni,Lei Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RxnNano是一个0.5B参数的化学反应预测模型，通过强调化学理解而非规模扩展，在多个基准测试中显著优于更大的模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数据驱动模型过度强调参数和数据集规模，缺乏对反应表示基本挑战的解决，未能捕捉深层化学直觉如反应常识和拓扑原子映射逻辑。核心挑战在于将这些化学知识注入模型中。

Method: 提出统一框架，包含四个关键创新：(1) 潜在化学一致性目标，将反应建模为连续化学流形上的运动；(2) 分层认知课程，通过渐进阶段训练模型；(3) 原子映射置换不变性，强制模型学习不变关系拓扑；(4) 结构化计划推理提升LLMs性能。

Result: 0.5B参数的RxnNano显著优于十倍大的微调LLMs（>7B）和所有领域基线，在严格基准测试中实现23.5%的Top-1准确率提升，无需测试时增强。

Conclusion: 通过优先考虑化学理解而非规模扩展，RxnNano证明了紧凑模型在化学反应预测任务上的卓越性能，为药物发现和合成规划提供了更有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Chemical reaction prediction is pivotal for accelerating drug discovery and synthesis planning. Despite advances in data-driven models, current approaches are hindered by an overemphasis on parameter and dataset scaling. Some methods coupled with evaluation techniques that bypass fundamental challenges in reaction representation and fail to capture deep chemical intuition like reaction common sense and {topological atom mapping logic}. We argue that the core challenge lies in instilling these knowledge into the models. To this end, we propose a unified framework that prioritizes chemical understanding over scale through three key innovations: (1) a {Latent Chemical Consistency} objective that models reactions as movements on a continuous chemical manifold, ensuring reversible and physically plausible transformations; (2) a {Hierarchical Cognitive Curriculum} that trains the model through progressive stages, from syntax mastery to semantic reasoning, building robust chemical intuition; (3) {Atom-Map Permutation Invariance (AMPI)}, which force the model to learn invariant relational topology and balance multi-task learning. (4)and structured plan-based reasoning to improve the performance of the LLMs. Our compact {0.5B-parameter model}, \textbf{RxnNano} significantly outperforms fine-tuned LLMs ten times larger (>7B) and all the domain baselines, achieving a 23.5\% Top-1 accuracy improvement on rigorous benchmarks without test-time augmentation. https://github.com/rlisml/RxnNano.

</details>


### [37] [ATPO: Adaptive Tree Policy Optimization for Multi-Turn Medical Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02216)
*Ruike Cao,Shaojie Bai,Fugen Yao,Liang Dong,Jian Xu,Li Xiao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出ATPO算法解决医疗对话中信息不完整问题，通过不确定性感知的自适应树策略优化，在三个医疗对话基准上显著超越基线，Qwen3-8B模型超越GPT-4o


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗多轮对话中有效信息获取对准确诊断至关重要，但现有LLM对齐方法在处理交互不确定性时面临挑战。传统RL方法如GRPO存在长视野信用分配困难，PPO在该场景下价值估计不稳定，需要新的优化算法

Method: 提出不确定性感知的自适应树策略优化(ATPO)算法：1) 使用贝尔曼误差和动作价值方差的复合指标量化不确定性；2) 自适应分配rollout预算到高不确定性状态；3) 引入不确定性引导的剪枝机制减少rollout次数；4) 采用异步搜索架构和KV缓存重用最大化推理吞吐量

Result: 在三个公开医疗对话基准上的实验表明，ATPO算法显著优于多个强基线。Qwen3-8B模型最终超越更大的GPT-4o模型，准确率提升+0.92%

Conclusion: ATPO算法通过不确定性感知的自适应树策略优化，有效解决了医疗对话中信息不完整问题，实现了更准确的价值估计和更高效的探索，在计算效率和性能上都取得了显著提升

Abstract: Effective information seeking in multi-turn medical dialogues is critical for accurate diagnosis, especially when dealing with incomplete information. Aligning Large Language Models (LLMs) for these interactive scenarios is challenging due to the uncertainty inherent in user-agent interactions, which we formulate as a Hierarchical Markov Decision Process (H-MDP). While conventional Reinforcement Learning (RL) methods like Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) struggle with long-horizon credit assignment and Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) suffers from unstable value estimation in this context, we propose a novel uncertainty-aware Adaptive Tree Policy Optimization (ATPO) algorithm. Our method adaptively allocates the rollout budget to states with high uncertainty, quantified by a composite metric of Bellman error and action-value variance. This strategy enables more accurate value estimation, while fostering more efficient and diverse exploration. To mitigate the high computational cost of tree-based RL, we introduce two key optimizations: an uncertainty-guided pruning mechanism to minimize the number of rollouts, and an asynchronous search architecture that leverages KV cache reuse to maximize inference throughput. Extensive experiments on three public medical dialogue benchmarks demonstrate that our algorithm significantly outperforms several strong baselines, culminating in Qwen3-8B model surpassing the much larger GPT-4o ($+0.92\%$ accuracy).

</details>


### [38] [From Heuristic Selection to Automated Algorithm Design: LLMs Benefit from Strong Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02792)
*Qi Huang,Furong Ye,Ananta Shahane,Thomas Bäck,Niki van Stein*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LLM驱动的算法设计通过高质量算法代码示例和基准算法引导可显著提升黑盒优化性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要关注LLM在解决特定问题上的有效性，搜索策略主要依赖自适应提示设计，缺乏对提示中算法代码示例质量影响的深入分析

Method: 通过分析提示对LLM生成算法代码的token-wise归因，发现高质量算法代码示例能显著提升性能；提出利用先验基准算法引导LLM驱动的优化，并在pbo和bbob两个黑盒优化基准上验证

Result: 在伪布尔优化套件(pbo)和黑盒优化套件(bbob)上展示了优越性能，证明基准算法引导能有效提升LLM驱动黑盒优化的效率和鲁棒性

Conclusion: 整合基准研究对增强LLM驱动黑盒优化方法的效率和鲁棒性具有重要价值，高质量算法示例和基准算法引导是提升性能的关键因素

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have already been widely adopted for automated algorithm design, demonstrating strong abilities in generating and evolving algorithms across various fields. Existing work has largely focused on examining their effectiveness in solving specific problems, with search strategies primarily guided by adaptive prompt designs. In this paper, through investigating the token-wise attribution of the prompts to LLM-generated algorithmic codes, we show that providing high-quality algorithmic code examples can substantially improve the performance of the LLM-driven optimization. Building upon this insight, we propose leveraging prior benchmark algorithms to guide LLM-driven optimization and demonstrate superior performance on two black-box optimization benchmarks: the pseudo-Boolean optimization suite (pbo) and the black-box optimization suite (bbob). Our findings highlight the value of integrating benchmarking studies to enhance both efficiency and robustness of the LLM-driven black-box optimization methods.

</details>


### [39] [Joint Optimization of Model Partitioning and Resource Allocation for Anti-Jamming Collaborative Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02579)
*Mengru Wu,Jiawei Li,Jiaqi Wei,Bin Lyu,Kai-Kit Wong,Hyundong Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种抗干扰的DNN设备-边缘协同推理系统，通过联合优化计算资源分配、设备发射功率和DNN划分来最大化延迟与准确率收益，并设计了基于交替优化的高效算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着DNN推理在资源受限设备上的计算需求增加，基于DNN划分的设备-边缘协同推理成为一种有前景的范式。然而，中间特征数据的传输容易受到恶意干扰攻击，这会显著降低整体推理性能。因此需要设计抗干扰的协同推理系统来应对这一威胁。

Method: 首先通过数据回归分析干扰和DNN划分对推理准确率的影响。然后构建以最大化延迟与准确率收益为目标、受推理准确率和计算资源约束的优化问题。针对这个混合整数非线性规划问题，提出基于交替优化的高效算法：将问题分解为三个子问题，分别通过KKT条件、凸优化方法和量子遗传算法求解，联合优化计算资源分配、设备发射功率和DNN划分。

Result: 大量仿真实验表明，所提出的方案在延迟与准确率收益方面优于基线方法，验证了所提抗干扰协同推理系统的有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究成功设计了一种抗干扰的DNN设备-边缘协同推理系统，通过联合优化多个关键参数，在存在恶意干扰的情况下有效提升了系统的延迟与准确率收益，为资源受限环境下的可靠DNN推理提供了解决方案。

Abstract: With the increasing computational demands of deep neural network (DNN) inference on resource-constrained devices, DNN partitioning-based device-edge collaborative inference has emerged as a promising paradigm. However, the transmission of intermediate feature data is vulnerable to malicious jamming, which significantly degrades the overall inference performance. To counter this threat, this letter focuses on an anti-jamming collaborative inference system in the presence of a malicious jammer. In this system, a DNN model is partitioned into two distinct segments, which are executed by wireless devices and edge servers, respectively. We first analyze the effects of jamming and DNN partitioning on inference accuracy via data regression. Based on this, our objective is to maximize the system's revenue of delay and accuracy (RDA) under inference accuracy and computing resource constraints by jointly optimizing computation resource allocation, devices' transmit power, and DNN partitioning. To address the mixed-integer nonlinear programming problem, we propose an efficient alternating optimization-based algorithm, which decomposes the problem into three subproblems that are solved via Karush-Kuhn-Tucker conditions, convex optimization methods, and a quantum genetic algorithm, respectively. Extensive simulations demonstrate that our proposed scheme outperforms baselines in terms of RDA.

</details>


### [40] [Is Retraining-Free Enough? The Necessity of Router Calibration for Efficient MoE Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02217)
*Sieun Hyeon,Jaeyoung Do*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Router Knowledge Distillation方法，通过仅更新路由器的轻量级校准来解决MoE模型压缩后的性能退化问题，无需重新训练专家参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE模型虽然能高效扩展容量，但其巨大的参数量在部署时造成内存瓶颈。现有的免重训练压缩方法（专家剪枝、编辑、合并）会导致性能持续退化，主要原因是压缩后专家参数改变但路由器未更新，造成路由器-专家不匹配问题。

Method: 提出Router Knowledge Distillation方法：1）将免重训练MoE压缩分为专家剪枝、专家编辑、专家合并三种范式；2）仅更新路由器参数（占总参数极小比例），通过蒸馏原始模型在未标记校准数据上的下一个token分布来校准路由器；3）避免更新专家参数，保持压缩效率。

Result: 实验表明：1）Router KD在所有三种压缩范式中都能实现一致的性能恢复；2）在细粒度MoE（专家多且小）中效果更显著，因为其路由决策边界更复杂；3）相比粗粒度MoE，细粒度MoE获得更大的性能提升。

Conclusion: 有效的免重训练MoE压缩应避免更新专家参数，同时允许轻量级路由器校准。Router KD通过仅更新路由器并蒸馏原始模型知识，有效解决了压缩后的路由器-专家不匹配问题，显著提升了压缩模型的性能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models scale capacity efficiently, but their massive parameter footprint creates a deployment-time memory bottleneck. We organize retraining-free MoE compression into three paradigms - Expert Pruning, Expert Editing, and Expert Merging - and show that persistent post-compression degradation largely stems from a neglected factor: router-expert mismatch when experts are changed but the router is left untouched. We argue that effective retraining-free compression should avoid updating expert parameters while allowing lightweight router calibration. To this end, we propose Router Knowledge Distillation (Router KD), which updates only a tiny fraction of parameters (the router) by distilling the original model's next-token distribution on unlabeled calibration data. Experiments across representative methods in all three paradigms demonstrate consistent performance recovery, with substantially larger gains in fine-grained MoEs (many small experts) than in coarse-grained MoEs due to their more complex routing decision boundaries.

</details>
