Finally, we provide GPU implementation of the proposed methodologies using custom CUDA kernels for SOC projection steps and cuBLAS GEMM chains for feedback gain updates. We validate the performance of cuNRTO through simulated experiments on unicycle, quadcopter, and Franka manipulator models, demonstrating speedup up to 139.6$\times$.

</details>


### [120] [A Novel Modular Cable-Driven Soft Robotic Arm with Multi-Segment Reconfigurability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02468)
*Moeen Ul Islam,Cheng Ouyang,Xinda Qi,Azlan Zahid,Xiaobo Tan,Dong Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种模块化、缆绳驱动的软体机器人手臂，具有多段可重构性，通过堆叠模块实现工作空间扩展，并研究了硅胶刚度对性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种可扩展、可重构的软体机器人手臂系统，能够适应不同的结构要求和应用需求，同时探索材料刚度对软体执行器性能的影响。

Method: 采用模块化设计，使用软硅胶材料制造，嵌入肌腱导向通道并采用保护性双螺旋肌腱结构，实现独立段控制，通过实验研究不同刚度硅胶的性能表现。

Result: 模块化堆叠显著扩展了可达工作空间：三段配置相比单段实现了平面工作空间面积13倍增加和体积38.9倍增加；硅胶刚度存在明显权衡：较软硅胶提高弯曲灵活性，较硬硅胶提高结构刚度和负载稳定性。

Conclusion: 模块化可重构软体机器人手臂具有显著的工作空间扩展潜力，通过调整硅胶刚度可以在柔顺性和强度之间取得平衡，为可扩展、可重构的软体机器人系统配置提供了有效方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a novel, modular, cable-driven soft robotic arm featuring multi-segment reconfigurability. The proposed architecture enables a stackable system with independent segment control, allowing scalable adaptation to diverse structural and application requirements. The system is fabricated from soft silicone material and incorporates embedded tendon-routing channels with a protective dual-helical tendon structure. Experimental results showed that modular stacking substantially expanded the reachable workspace: relative to the single-segment arm, the three-segment configuration achieved up to a 13-fold increase in planar workspace area and a 38.9-fold increase in workspace volume. Furthermore, this study investigated the effect of silicone stiffness on actuator performance. The results revealed a clear trade-off between compliance and stiffness: softer silicone improved bending flexibility, while stiffer silicone improved structural rigidity and load-bearing stability. These results highlight the potential of stiffness tuning to balance compliance and strength for configuring scalable, reconfigurable soft robotic arms.

</details>


### [121] [How to Peel with a Knife: Aligning Fine-Grained Manipulation with Human Preference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03280)
*Toru Lin,Shuying Deng,Zhao-Heng Yin,Pieter Abbeel,Jitendra Malik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个两阶段学习框架解决接触丰富、力敏感且具有隐式成功标准的操作任务，以刀具削皮为代表性案例，通过模仿学习和基于偏好的微调实现高性能和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多关键操作任务（如食物准备、手术、手工艺）对自主机器人仍然难以解决。这些任务不仅具有接触丰富、力敏感的动态特性，还具有"隐式"成功标准：任务质量是连续且主观的（如土豆削皮的好坏），使得定量评估和奖励工程变得困难。

Method: 采用两阶段学习框架：1）通过力感知数据收集和模仿学习学习鲁棒的初始策略，实现跨物体变化的泛化；2）通过基于偏好的微调优化策略，使用结合定量任务指标和定性人类反馈的学习奖励模型，使策略行为与人类对任务质量的概念对齐。

Result: 仅使用50-200个削皮轨迹，系统在黄瓜、苹果、土豆等挑战性农产品上实现了超过90%的平均成功率，通过基于偏好的微调性能提升高达40%。在单个农产品类别上训练的策略对未见过的同类实例和分布外不同类别农产品表现出强大的零样本泛化能力，同时保持超过90%的成功率。

Conclusion: 该框架成功解决了具有隐式成功标准的接触丰富操作任务，通过结合模仿学习和基于偏好的微调，实现了高性能和强大的泛化能力，为食品准备等复杂操作任务的自主化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Many essential manipulation tasks - such as food preparation, surgery, and craftsmanship - remain intractable for autonomous robots. These tasks are characterized not only by contact-rich, force-sensitive dynamics, but also by their "implicit" success criteria: unlike pick-and-place, task quality in these domains is continuous and subjective (e.g. how well a potato is peeled), making quantitative evaluation and reward engineering difficult. We present a learning framework for such tasks, using peeling with a knife as a representative example. Our approach follows a two-stage pipeline: first, we learn a robust initial policy via force-aware data collection and imitation learning, enabling generalization across object variations; second, we refine the policy through preference-based finetuning using a learned reward model that combines quantitative task metrics with qualitative human feedback, aligning policy behavior with human notions of task quality. Using only 50-200 peeling trajectories, our system achieves over 90% average success rates on challenging produce including cucumbers, apples, and potatoes, with performance improving by up to 40% through preference-based finetuning. Remarkably, policies trained on a single produce category exhibit strong zero-shot generalization to unseen in-category instances and to out-of-distribution produce from different categories while maintaining over 90% success rates.

</details>


### [122] [Generative adversarial imitation learning for robot swarms: Learning from human demonstrations and trained policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02783)
*Mattes Kraus,Jonas Kuckling*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 基于生成对抗模仿学习的群体机器人框架，从人类演示中学习集体行为，并在真实TurtleBot 4机器人上验证


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有群体机器人模仿学习大多基于现有策略的轨迹演示，本文旨在开发能够从人类演示中学习集体行为的框架

Method: 基于生成对抗模仿学习（GAIL）的框架，从手动演示和PPO训练策略的演示中学习，在六个不同任务中评估

Result: 模仿学习能够学习到具有定性意义的行为，性能与提供的演示相当；在真实TurtleBot 4机器人上部署时，行为保持视觉可识别特征，性能与仿真结果可比

Conclusion: 提出的生成对抗模仿学习框架能够有效从人类演示中学习群体机器人集体行为，并在真实机器人上成功部署验证

Abstract: In imitation learning, robots are supposed to learn from demonstrations of the desired behavior. Most of the work in imitation learning for swarm robotics provides the demonstrations as rollouts of an existing policy. In this work, we provide a framework based on generative adversarial imitation learning that aims to learn collective behaviors from human demonstrations. Our framework is evaluated across six different missions, learning both from manual demonstrations and demonstrations derived from a PPO-trained policy. Results show that the imitation learning process is able to learn qualitatively meaningful behaviors that perform similarly well as the provided demonstrations. Additionally, we deploy the learned policies on a swarm of TurtleBot 4 robots in real-robot experiments. The exhibited behaviors preserved their visually recognizable character and their performance is comparable to the one achieved in simulation.

</details>


### [123] [COLREGs Compliant Collision Avoidance and Grounding Prevention for Autonomous Marine Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02484)
*Mayur S. Patil,Nataraj Sudharsan,Veneela Ammula,Jude Tomdio,Jin Wang,Michael Kei,Sivakumar Rathinam,Prabhakar R. Pagilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种统一的海上自主水面船舶运动规划方法，通过凸优化整合避碰、COLREGs合规和搁浅预防约束，实现实时、规则合规的自主导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上自主水面船舶（MASS）在解决船员短缺、提高航行安全和运营效率方面具有潜力，但在拥挤水域的实际部署仍面临挑战。需要开发能够在动态海事条件下安全运行且符合国际规则的运动规划策略。

Method: 采用凸优化方法，整合基于速度障碍的碰撞约束、基于COLREGs的方向约束和基于测深数据的搁浅约束。扩展经典VO方法以考虑目标船舶位置和速度估计的不确定性，使用整数线性规划将非凸的浅水区域近似为凸几何形状，从而将搁浅约束纳入运动规划。

Result: 仿真结果表明，该方法在多船相遇场景中能够生成安全且符合规则的操作，证明了其在实时自主海事导航中的适用性。优化生成的输入速度既满足避碰、规则合规、运动学限制和搁浅预防要求，又具有计算效率。

Conclusion: 提出的统一运动规划方法能够为MASS提供实时、安全、规则合规的导航能力，特别适用于拥挤水域，为解决自主船舶在实际部署中的关键挑战提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Maritime Autonomous Surface Ships (MASS) are increasingly regarded as a promising solution to address crew shortages, improve navigational safety, and improve operational efficiency in the maritime industry. Nevertheless, the reliable deployment of MASS in real-world environments remains a significant challenge, particularly in congested waters where the majority of maritime accidents occur. This emphasizes the need for safe and regulation-aware motion planning strategies for MASS that are capable of operating under dynamic maritime conditions. This paper presents a unified motion planning method for MASS that achieves real time collision avoidance, compliance with International Regulations for Preventing Collisions at Sea (COLREGs), and grounding prevention. The proposed work introduces a convex optimization method that integrates velocity obstacle-based (VO) collision constraints, COLREGs-based directional constraints, and bathymetry-based grounding constraints to generate computationally efficient, rule-compliant optimal velocity selection. To enhance robustness, the classical VO method is extended to consider uncertainty in the position and velocity estimates of the target vessel. Unnavigable shallow water regions obtained from bathymetric data, which are inherently nonconvex, are approximated via convex geometries using a integer linear programming (ILP), allowing grounding constraints to be incorporated into the motion planning. The resulting optimization generates optimal and dynamically feasible input velocities that meet collision avoidance, regulatory compliance, kinodynamic limits, and grounding prevention requirements. Simulation results involving multi-vessel encounters demonstrate the effectiveness of the proposed method in producing safe and regulation-compliant maneuvers, highlighting the suitability of the proposed approach for real time autonomous maritime navigation.

</details>


### [124] [A Robust Simulation Framework for Verification and Validation of Autonomous Maritime Navigation in Adverse Weather and Constrained Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02487)
*Mayur S. Patil,Nataraj Sudharsan,Anthony S. Saaiby,JiaChang Xing,Keliang Pan,Veneela Ammula,Jude Tomdio,Jin Wang,Michael Kei,Heonyong Kang,Sivakumar Rathinam,Prabhakar R. Pagilla*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个增强的虚拟仿真框架，用于在真实海洋环境中验证和确认海上自主水面船舶，特别关注天气和海底地形对自主导航性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 海上自主水面船舶(MASS)需要严格的验证和确认(V&V)，特别是在极端和安全关键场景下。现有方法难以在真实环境中全面测试各种天气和地形条件，需要开发能够模拟恶劣天气和高分辨率海底地形的虚拟仿真框架。

Method: 开发了一个增强的虚拟仿真框架，包含高保真环境建模套件，能够模拟雨、雾和波浪动力学等恶劣天气条件。天气参数影响海况特征、感知和传感系统，导致位置和速度不确定性、能见度降低和态势感知退化。同时集成了来自美国主要港口的高分辨率海底地形数据，实现深度感知导航、搁浅预防能力，并评估在浅水或受限水域的船舶可控性。

Result: 该框架提供了广泛的配置能力，能够在广泛的海事条件下进行系统测试，包括在实际试验中不切实际或不安全的场景。通过参数化天气因素和集成高分辨率海底数据，能够全面评估MASS在各种环境条件下的导航性能。

Conclusion: 所提出的增强虚拟仿真框架能够支持MASS的验证和确认，特别是在考虑天气和海底地形影响的真实海洋环境中。该框架能够模拟实际试验中难以复现的极端条件，为MASS的安全可靠部署提供了重要的测试平台。

Abstract: Maritime Autonomous Surface Ships (MASS) have emerged as a promising solution to enhance navigational safety, operational efficiency, and long-term cost effectiveness. However, their reliable deployment requires rigorous verification and validation (V\&V) under various environmental conditions, including extreme and safety-critical scenarios. This paper presents an enhanced virtual simulation framework to support the V\&V of MASS in realistic maritime environments, with particular emphasis on the influence of weather and bathymetry on autonomous navigation performance. The framework incorporates a high-fidelity environmental modeling suite capable of simulating adverse weather conditions such as rain, fog, and wave dynamics. The key factors that affect weather, such as rain and visibility, are parameterized to affect sea-state characteristics, perception, and sensing systems, resulting in position and velocity uncertainty, reduced visibility, and degraded situational awareness. Furthermore, high-resolution bathymetric data from major U.S. ports are integrated to enable depth-aware navigation, grounding prevention capabilities, and evaluation of vessel controllability in shallow or confined waterways. The proposed framework offers extensive configurability, enabling systematic testing in a wide spectrum of maritime conditions, including scenarios that are impractical or unsafe to replicate in real-world trials, thus supporting the V\&V of MASS.

</details>


### [125] [Instant and Reversible Adhesive-free Bonding Between Silicones and Glossy Papers for Soft Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02500)
*Takumi Shibuya,Kazuya Murakami,Akitsu Shigetou,Jun Shintake*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种无粘合剂、溶剂可逆的硅胶-光面纸粘接方法，通过简单接触形成强界面，支持可拆卸重用和快速重新设计


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统硅胶与非延展材料集成方法依赖不可逆粘合剂或嵌入工艺，劳动密集且难以修改，需要更简单、可逆的粘接方案

Method: 开发硅胶-光面纸粘接技术，通过接触形成界面，利用乙醇浸泡实现完全拆卸和重新组装，研究表面官能团和硅胶模量的影响

Result: 界面剪切强度>61 kPa，可完全拆卸和重新组装而不损失性能，软体执行器性能等于或优于传统嵌入层设计，支持收缩、弯曲和扭转等多种驱动模式

Conclusion: SGB为软体机器人快速原型制作提供了可扩展、可持续的平台，简化了制造过程，支持重用和快速迭代

Abstract: Integrating silicone with non-extensible materials is a common strategy used in the fabrication of fluidically-driven soft actuators, yet conventional approaches often rely on irreversible adhesives or embedding processes that are labor-intensive and difficult to modify. This work presents silicone-glossy paper bonding (SGB), a rapid, adhesive-free, and solvent-reversible bonding approach that forms robust silicone-paper interfaces simply through contact. The SGB interface withstands high mechanical loads (shear strength > 61 kPa) and can be fully detached and reassembled via ethanol immersion without loss of performance, enabling component reuse and rapid redesign. Characterization studies indicate that surface functional groups primarily govern adhesion on the glossy paper and the modulus of the silicone, while durability and environmental response clarify the conditions for reversible debonding. The results further suggest a synergistic interaction of hydrogen bonding and oligomer diffusion, yielding strong yet reconfigurable adhesion. Soft actuators fabricated using SGB design exhibit equal or greater performance compared to conventional embedded-layer design and enable programmable actuation modes, including contraction, bending, and twisting. By simplifying fabrication while supporting reuse and rapid iteration, SGB offers a scalable and sustainable platform for rapid prototyping in soft robotics.

</details>


### [126] [Learning Object-Centric Spatial Reasoning for Sequential Manipulation in Cluttered Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02511)
*Chrisantus Eze,Ryan C Julian,Christopher Crick*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Unveiler框架通过解耦空间推理与动作执行，专门用于密集杂乱环境中的机器人抓取任务，在模拟和真实场景中均表现出色。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模端到端模型在密集杂乱环境中的物体抓取任务上存在数据效率低和模块化不足的问题，需要更专门化、解耦的系统架构。

Method: 提出Unveiler框架，包含轻量级Transformer空间关系编码器(SRE)进行高层空间推理，识别关键障碍物，然后由旋转不变动作解码器执行移除操作。SRE采用两阶段训练：启发式演示的模仿学习初始化，PPO微调。

Result: 在模拟环境中，部分遮挡场景成功率97.6%，完全遮挡场景90.0%。SRE的空间推理能力可零样本迁移到真实场景，整个系统仅需几何工作空间标定，无需重新训练学习组件。

Conclusion: 专门化、解耦的架构在复杂操作任务中具有优势，证明了基于物体的空间推理在密集杂乱环境机器人抓取中的有效性。

Abstract: Robotic manipulation in cluttered environments presents a critical challenge for automation. Recent large-scale, end-to-end models demonstrate impressive capabilities but often lack the data efficiency and modularity required for retrieving objects in dense clutter. In this work, we argue for a paradigm of specialized, decoupled systems and present Unveiler, a framework that explicitly separates high-level spatial reasoning from low-level action execution. Unveiler's core is a lightweight, transformer-based Spatial Relationship Encoder (SRE) that sequentially identifies the most critical obstacle for removal. This discrete decision is then passed to a rotation-invariant Action Decoder for execution. We demonstrate that this decoupled architecture is not only more computationally efficient in terms of parameter count and inference time, but also significantly outperforms both classic end-to-end policies and modern, large-model-based baselines in retrieving targets from dense clutter. The SRE is trained in two stages: imitation learning from heuristic demonstrations provides sample-efficient initialization, after which PPO fine-tuning enables the policy to discover removal strategies that surpass the heuristic in dense clutter. Our results, achieving up to 97.6\% success in partially occluded and 90.0\% in fully occluded scenarios in simulation, make a case for the power of specialized, object-centric reasoning in complex manipulation tasks. Additionally, we demonstrate that the SRE's spatial reasoning transfers zero-shot to real scenes, and validate the full system on a physical robot requiring only geometric workspace calibration; no learned components are retrained.

</details>


### [127] [PathSpace: Rapid continuous map approximation for efficient SLAM using B-Splines in constrained environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02538)
*Aduen Benjumea,Andrew Bradley,Alexander Rast,Matthias Rolf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PathSpace：一种基于连续B样条表示环境的新型语义SLAM框架，在自主赛车场景中利用赛道先验知识实现紧凑表示，在保持精度的同时显著减少资源消耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语义SLAM技术主要依赖环境的密集几何表示，限制了基于上下文约束的应用能力。需要一种更紧凑、能进行概率推理的表示方法，以在资源受限场景中实现高效的环境建模。

Method: 提出PathSpace框架，使用连续B样条以紧凑方式表示环境，同时维护连续概率密度函数进行概率推理。利用B样条在SLAM中的多重优势，对离散稀疏环境进行插值和拟合。

Result: 在自主赛车场景中测试，利用预定义的赛道特性生成显著简化的环境表示，与传统地标方法相比在可比精度水平下大幅减少表示复杂度，展示了在最小精度损失下限制系统资源使用的潜力。

Conclusion: PathSpace通过B样条表示提供了一种紧凑且概率完备的语义SLAM方法，特别适用于具有结构化先验知识的场景，为资源受限的自主系统提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Simultaneous Localization and Mapping (SLAM) plays a crucial role in enabling autonomous vehicles to navigate previously unknown environments.
  Semantic SLAM mostly extends visual SLAM, leveraging the higher density information available to reason about the environment in a more human-like manner. This allows for better decision making by exploiting prior structural knowledge of the environment, usually in the form of labels. Current semantic SLAM techniques still mostly rely on a dense geometric representation of the environment, limiting their ability to apply constraints based on context. We propose PathSpace, a novel semantic SLAM framework that uses continuous B-splines to represent the environment in a compact manner, while also maintaining and reasoning through the continuous probability density functions required for probabilistic reasoning. This system applies the multiple strengths of B-splines in the context of SLAM to interpolate and fit otherwise discrete sparse environments. We test this framework in the context of autonomous racing, where we exploit pre-specified track characteristics to produce significantly reduced representations at comparable levels of accuracy to traditional landmark based methods and demonstrate its potential in limiting the resources used by a system with minimal accuracy loss.

</details>


### [128] [Give me scissors: Collision-Free Dual-Arm Surgical Assistive Robot for Instrument Delivery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02553)
*Xuejin Luo,Shiquan Sun,Runshi Zhang,Ruizhi Zhang,Junchen Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于视觉语言模型的免碰撞双臂手术辅助机器人，能够根据外科医生指令零样本生成器械抓取和递送轨迹，在动态环境中实现自主避障


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人刷手护士依赖预定义路径递送手术器械，限制了泛化能力并在动态环境中存在安全风险，需要更智能、安全的解决方案

Method: 使用视觉语言模型根据外科医生指令零样本生成机器人抓取和递送轨迹；提出实时障碍物最小距离感知方法，并集成到统一的二次规划框架中，确保动态环境中的反应式避障和自碰撞预防

Result: 实验验证表明，所提出的机器人系统在手术器械递送中达到83.33%的成功率，同时在所有试验中保持平滑、无碰撞的运动

Conclusion: 该方法通过结合视觉语言模型和实时避障框架，实现了在动态手术环境中安全、自主的手术器械递送，为机器人刷手护士提供了更通用、安全的解决方案

Abstract: During surgery, scrub nurses are required to frequently deliver surgical instruments to surgeons, which can lead to physical fatigue and decreased focus. Robotic scrub nurses provide a promising solution that can replace repetitive tasks and enhance efficiency. Existing research on robotic scrub nurses relies on predefined paths for instrument delivery, which limits their generalizability and poses safety risks in dynamic environments. To address these challenges, we present a collision-free dual-arm surgical assistive robot capable of performing instrument delivery. A vision-language model is utilized to automatically generate the robot's grasping and delivery trajectories in a zero-shot manner based on surgeons' instructions. A real-time obstacle minimum distance perception method is proposed and integrated into a unified quadratic programming framework. This framework ensures reactive obstacle avoidance and self-collision prevention during the dual-arm robot's autonomous movement in dynamic environments. Extensive experimental validations demonstrate that the proposed robotic system achieves an 83.33% success rate in surgical instrument delivery while maintaining smooth, collision-free movement throughout all trials. The project page and source code are available at https://give-me-scissors.github.io/.

</details>


### [129] [Wukong-Omni: Design, Modeling and Control of a Multi-mode Robot for Air, Land, and Underwater Exploration with All-in-One Propulsion Unit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02602)
*Yufan Liu,Rixi Yu,Junjie Li,Yishuai Zeng,Zhenting Wen,Cheng Li,Haifei Zhu,Shikang Lian,Wei Meng,Fumin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Wukong-Omni是一种新型多模式机器人，采用统一推进系统实现陆地、空中和水下三域操作，通过创新的机械设计重用电机并提升推力，实验显示推进效率提升100%，最大推力增加150%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在洪水灾害救援场景中，部分淹没的建筑阻碍了空中机器人进入低层区域，限制了任务执行效果。现有机器人难以在陆地、空中和水下三域间无缝切换操作，需要开发能够跨越这些领域的新型机器人系统。

Method: 1. 提出Wukong-Omni多模式机器人，采用统一推进系统实现三域操作；2. 创新机械设计实现电机重用并提升推力生成；3. 通过仿真和水槽实验优化效率和峰值推力；4. 开发三域动态模型；5. 提出统一的跨域控制框架。

Result: 1. 推进效率相比直接安装方法提升100%；2. 最大推力增加150%；3. 实验验证了稳定的运动性能和流畅的跨域转换；4. 室外实验展示了在实际环境中的鲁棒性和适应性。

Conclusion: Wukong-Omni通过创新的统一推进系统和机械设计，成功实现了陆地、空中和水下三域的无缝操作，显著提升了洪水灾害救援等场景中的任务执行能力，为跨域机器人系统提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: In flood disaster rescue scenarios, partially submerged buildings prevent aerial robots from accessing lower levels, limiting mission effectiveness. To address this challenge, this paper presents Wukong-Omni, a novel multimode robot capable of operating across land, air, and underwater using a unified propulsion system. The system is enabled by an innovative mechanical design that allows motor reuse and improves thrust generation. Efficiency and peak thrust are enhanced through simulation and tank-based optimization. Experimental results show a 100 percent improvement in propulsion efficiency and a 150 percent increase in maximum thrust compared with direct installation methods. Dynamic models for the three operating domains are developed, and a unified cross-domain control framework is proposed. Comprehensive experiments validate stable locomotion and smooth transition across domains. Outdoor experiments further demonstrate robustness and adaptability in real-world environments.

</details>


### [130] [Uni-Skill: Building Self-Evolving Skill Repository for Generalizable Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02623)
*Senwei Xie,Yuntian Zhang,Ruiping Wang,Xilin Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Uni-Skill是一个统一的技能中心框架，支持技能感知规划并促进自动技能演化，通过SkillFolder从大规模机器人视频构建技能库，实现无需手动干预的自适应规划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有技能中心方法依赖固定的技能库，限制了在新任务中的适应性，需要手动干预。为了解决这一问题，需要开发能够自动演化技能库、支持自适应规划的系统

Method: 提出Uni-Skill框架，包含技能感知规划模块和SkillFolder技能库。SkillFolder从大规模非结构化机器人视频构建，采用类似VerbNet的分层技能分类法，自动标注演示数据。当现有技能不足时，系统请求新技能实现，通过离线结构检索实现少样本技能推断

Result: 在仿真和真实世界环境中进行的全面实验验证了Uni-Skill相对于现有VLM-based技能中心方法的SOTA性能，展示了其先进的推理能力和在广泛新任务中的强零样本泛化能力

Conclusion: Uni-Skill通过自动技能演化和分层技能分类法，将技能获取范式从低效的手动标注转变为高效的离线结构检索，实现了自适应规划和强泛化能力

Abstract: While skill-centric approaches leverage foundation models to enhance generalization in compositional tasks, they often rely on fixed skill libraries, limiting adaptability to new tasks without manual intervention. To address this, we propose Uni-Skill, a Unified Skill-centric framework that supports skill-aware planning and facilitates automatic skill evolution. Unlike prior methods that restrict planning to predefined skills, Uni-Skill requests for new skill implementations when existing ones are insufficient, ensuring adaptable planning with self-augmented skill library. To support automatic implementation of diverse skills requested by the planning module, we construct SkillFolder, a VerbNet-inspired repository derived from large-scale unstructured robotic videos. SkillFolder introduces a hierarchical skill taxonomy that captures diverse skill descriptions at multiple levels of abstraction. By populating this taxonomy with large-scale, automatically annotated demonstrations, Uni-Skill shifts the paradigm of skill acquisition from inefficient manual annotation to efficient offline structural retrieval. Retrieved examples provide semantic supervision over behavior patterns and fine-grained references for spatial trajectories, enabling few-shot skill inference without deployment-time demonstrations. Comprehensive experiments in both simulation and real-world settings verify the state-of-the-art performance of Uni-Skill over existing VLM-based skill-centric approaches, highlighting its advanced reasoning capabilities and strong zero-shot generalization across a wide range of novel tasks.

</details>


### [131] [Compositional Visual Planning via Inference-Time Diffusion Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02646)
*Yixin Zhang,Yunhao Luo,Utkarsh Aashu Mishra,Woo Chul Shin,Yongxin Chen,Danfei Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Tweedie估计边界一致性的训练免费组合扩散方法，用于长时程机器人规划，无需额外训练即可生成全局一致的计划


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型擅长短时程规划，但扩展到长时程任务面临计算限制和训练数据不足的挑战。现有组合方法在噪声数据空间中因分解假设失效而导致不稳定和全局计划不一致

Method: 将长时程规划建模为重叠视频块链式因子图的推理问题，使用预训练短时程视频扩散模型作为局部先验。在推理时通过同步和异步消息传递在Tweedie估计上强制边界一致性，无需额外训练

Result: 相比现有基线有显著改进，能够有效泛化到原始训练数据中未见过的起始-目标组合，实现全局一致的规划

Conclusion: 在Tweedie估计上强制边界一致性是实现稳定组合生成的关键，提出的训练免费框架通过消息传递机制解决了长时程规划中的一致性问题

Abstract: Diffusion models excel at short-horizon robot planning, yet scaling them to long-horizon tasks remains challenging due to computational constraints and limited training data. Existing compositional approaches stitch together short segments by separately denoising each component and averaging overlapping regions. However, this suffers from instability as the factorization assumption breaks down in noisy data space, leading to inconsistent global plans. We propose that the key to stable compositional generation lies in enforcing boundary agreement on the estimated clean data (Tweedie estimates) rather than on noisy intermediate states. Our method formulates long-horizon planning as inference over a chain-structured factor graph of overlapping video chunks, where pretrained short-horizon video diffusion models provide local priors. At inference time, we enforce boundary agreement through a novel combination of synchronous and asynchronous message passing that operates on Tweedie estimates, producing globally consistent guidance without requiring additional training. Our training-free framework demonstrates significant improvements over existing baselines, effectively generalizing to unseen start-goal combinations that were not present in the original training data. Project website: https://comp-visual-planning.github.io/

</details>


### [132] [Watch Your Step: Learning Semantically-Guided Locomotion in Cluttered Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02657)
*Denan Liang,Yuan Zhu,Ruimeng Liu,Thien-Minh Nguyen,Shenghai Yuan,Lihua Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SemLoco：一种结合语义地图和强化学习的框架，用于解决腿式机器人在密集杂乱环境中无法避免踩踏低矮障碍物的问题，通过两阶段RL和像素级立足点安全推理实现精确避障。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 腿式机器人在粗糙地形上表现出良好的移动性，但在杂乱环境中安全使用仍面临挑战。主要问题是无法避免踩踏低矮物体（如高价值小设备或地面电缆），这源于高层语义理解与底层控制的脱节，以及真实世界操作中高程地图的误差。

Method: 提出SemLoco强化学习框架：1）采用两阶段RL方法，结合软硬约束；2）进行像素级立足点安全推理，实现更精确的足部放置；3）集成语义地图分配可穿越性成本，而非仅依赖几何数据。

Result: SemLoco显著减少了碰撞，提高了敏感物体周围的安全性，在传统控制器可能造成损坏的情况下实现了可靠导航。实验结果进一步表明SemLoco可有效应用于更复杂的非结构化真实世界环境。

Conclusion: SemLoco通过结合语义理解和强化学习，解决了腿式机器人在密集杂乱环境中精确避障的关键问题，为机器人在复杂现实环境中的安全部署提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Although legged robots demonstrate impressive mobility on rough terrain, using them safely in cluttered environments remains a challenge. A key issue is their inability to avoid stepping on low-lying objects, such as high-cost small devices or cables on flat ground. This limitation arises from a disconnection between high-level semantic understanding and low-level control, combined with errors in elevation maps during real-world operation. To address this, we introduce SemLoco, a Reinforcement Learning (RL) framework designed to avoid obstacles precisely in densely cluttered environments. SemLoco uses a two-stage RL approach that combines both soft and hard constraints and performs pixel-wise foothold safety inference, enabling more accurate foot placement. Additionally, SemLoco integrates a semantic map to assign traversability costs rather than relying solely on geometric data. SemLoco significantly reduces collisions and improves safety around sensitive objects, enabling reliable navigation in situations where traditional controllers would likely cause damage. Experimental results further demonstrate that SemLoco can be effectively applied to more complex, unstructured real-world environments.

</details>


### [133] [IMR-LLM: Industrial Multi-Robot Task Planning and Program Generation using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02669)
*Xiangyu Su,Juzhan Xu,Oliver van Kaick,Kai Xu,Ruizhen Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出IMR-LLM框架，利用大语言模型进行工业多机器人任务规划与程序生成，通过构建析取图和确定性求解获得高效高层任务计划，再用过程树指导生成可执行低层程序，并在新基准IMR-Bench上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业场景中多机器人协作面临更严格的顺序约束和复杂依赖关系，现有基于大语言模型的机器人协调方法主要针对简单家庭和操作任务，难以应对工业制造的挑战。

Method: 提出IMR-LLM框架：1) 利用大语言模型辅助构建析取图，采用确定性求解方法获得可行高效的高层任务计划；2) 基于过程树指导大语言模型生成可执行的低层程序；3) 创建IMR-Bench基准，包含三个复杂度级别的多机器人工业任务。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法在所有评估指标上显著超越现有方法，证明了其在工业多机器人任务规划与程序生成方面的有效性。

Conclusion: IMR-LLM框架成功解决了工业场景中多机器人协作的严格顺序约束和复杂依赖问题，通过结合大语言模型的推理能力和确定性求解方法，实现了高效的任务规划与程序生成。

Abstract: In modern industrial production, multiple robots often collaborate to complete complex manufacturing tasks. Large language models (LLMs), with their strong reasoning capabilities, have shown potential in coordinating robots for simple household and manipulation tasks. However, in industrial scenarios, stricter sequential constraints and more complex dependencies within tasks present new challenges for LLMs. To address this, we propose IMR-LLM, a novel LLM-driven Industrial Multi-Robot task planning and program generation framework. Specifically, we utilize LLMs to assist in constructing disjunctive graphs and employ deterministic solving methods to obtain a feasible and efficient high-level task plan. Based on this, we use a process tree to guide LLMs to generate executable low-level programs. Additionally, we create IMR-Bench, a challenging benchmark that encompasses multi-robot industrial tasks across three levels of complexity. Experimental results indicate that our method significantly surpasses existing methods across all evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [134] [MMH-Planner: Multi-Mode Hybrid Trajectory Planning Method for UAV Efficient Flight Based on Real-Time Spatial Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02683)
*Yinghao Zhao,Chenguang Dai,Liang Lyu,Zhenchao Zhang,Chaozhen Lan,Hong Xie*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于实时环境感知的无人机多模式混合轨迹规划方法，通过动态选择最优规划模型来提升规划效率和质量


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前运动规划算法因策略不灵活和适应性弱，在规划效率上存在局限，需要更智能的轨迹规划方法

Method: 1) 面向目标的空间感知方法快速评估飞行安全；2) 多模式混合轨迹规划机制基于先验空间感知选择最优规划模型；3) 惰性重规划策略仅在必要时触发重规划

Result: 在仿真环境中全面对比实验表明，该方法在多个指标上优于现有SOTA算法，特别是在平均规划迭代次数和每次迭代计算成本方面表现最佳；通过自主研发的智能无人机平台进行了真实飞行实验验证

Conclusion: 提出的多模式混合轨迹规划方法有效提升了无人机轨迹规划的效率和适应性，为智能无人系统的自主操作提供了更优的解决方案

Abstract: Motion planning is a critical component of intelligent unmanned systems, enabling their complex autonomous operations. However, current planning algorithms still face limitations in planning efficiency due to inflexible strategies and weak adaptability. To address this, this paper proposes a multi-mode hybrid trajectory planning method for UAVs based on real-time environmental awareness, which dynamically selects the optimal planning model for high-quality trajectory generation in response to environmental changes. First, we introduce a goal-oriented spatial awareness method that rapidly assesses flight safety in the upcoming environments. Second, a multi-mode hybrid trajectory planning mechanism is proposed, which can enhance the planning efficiency by selecting the optimal planning model for trajectory generation based on prior spatial awareness. Finally, we design a lazy replanning strategy that triggers replanning only when necessary to reduce computational resource consumption while maintaining flight quality. To validate the performance of the proposed method, we conducted comprehensive comparative experiments in simulation environments. Results demonstrate that our approach outperforms existing state-of-the-art (SOTA) algorithms across multiple metrics, achieving the best performance particularly in terms of the average number of planning iterations and computational cost per iteration. Furthermore, the effectiveness of our approach is further verified through real-world flight experiments integrated with a self-developed intelligent UAV platform.

</details>


### [135] [Robust Tightly-Coupled Filter-Based Monocular Visual-Inertial State Estimation and Graph-Based Evaluation for Autonomous Drone Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02742)
*Maulana Bisyir Azhari,Donghun Han,SungJun Park,David Hyunchul Shim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ADR-VINS视觉惯性状态估计框架和ADR-FGO离线优化框架，用于自主无人机竞速，解决传统方法在高速机动下的感知退化问题


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机竞速需要计算高效且对高速机动下感知退化具有鲁棒性的状态估计。传统方法依赖四特征可见的PnP校正，在无GNSS的竞争环境中难以客观评估性能。

Method: 提出基于误差状态卡尔曼滤波的ADR-VINS框架，直接使用门角点像素重投影误差作为滤波创新项，最少只需两个可见角点；同时开发ADR-FGO离线因子图优化框架生成高精度参考轨迹。

Result: 在TII-RATM数据集上，ADR-VINS平均RMS平移误差0.134米，ADR-FGO为0.060米。在A2RL无人机锦标赛中，ADR-VINS在20.9米/秒高速飞行下保持稳定估计。

Conclusion: ADR-VINS通过直接像素重投影和鲁棒加权提高了计算效率和鲁棒性，ADR-FGO为无仪器环境提供了评估基准，两者结合为自主无人机竞速提供了完整解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous drone racing (ADR) demands state estimation that is simultaneously computationally efficient and resilient to the perceptual degradation experienced during extreme velocity and maneuvers. Traditional frameworks typically rely on conventional visual-inertial pipelines with loosely-coupled gate-based Perspective-n-Points (PnP) corrections that suffer from a rigid requirement for four visible features and information loss in intermediate steps. Furthermore, the absence of GNSS and Motion Capture systems in uninstrumented, competitive racing environments makes the objective evaluation of such systems remarkably difficult. To address these limitations, we propose ADR-VINS, a robust, monocular visual-inertial state estimation framework based on an Error-State Kalman Filter (ESKF) tailored for autonomous drone racing. Our approach integrates direct pixel reprojection errors from gate corners features as innovation terms within the filter. By bypassing intermediate PnP solvers, ADR-VINS maintains valid state updates with as few as two visible corners and utilizes robust reweighting instead of RANSAC-based schemes to handle outliers, enhancing computational efficiency. Furthermore, we introduce ADR-FGO, an offline Factor-Graph Optimization framework to generate high-fidelity reference trajectories that facilitate post-flight performance evaluation and analysis on uninstrumented, GNSS-denied environments. The proposed system is validated using TII-RATM dataset, where ADR-VINS achieves an average RMS translation error of 0.134 m, while ADR-FGO yields 0.060 m as a smoothing-based reference. Finally, ADR-VINS was successfully deployed in the A2RL Drone Championship Season 2, maintaining stable and robust estimation despite noisy detections during high-agility flight at top speeds of 20.9 m/s. We further utilize ADR-FGO for post-flight evaluation in uninstrumented racing environments.

</details>


### [136] [Agentic Self-Evolutionary Replanning for Embodied Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02772)
*Guoliang Li,Ruihua Han,Chengyang Li,He Li,Shuai Wang,Wenchao Ding,Hong Zhang,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SERP提出了一种自我进化重规划框架，通过运行时学习从失败经验中进化动作模型，结合图链式思维实现高效重规划，相比传统冻结模型方法具有更好的鲁棒性和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重规划方法通常冻结机器人的动作模型，无法从失败经验中学习进化，限制了在复杂环境中探索更好规划方案的能力。需要一种能够自我进化模型的重规划框架。

Method: 提出SERP框架：1）基于上下文学习与自动微分（ILAD）的智能体自我进化动作模型，实现自适应函数调整和全局参数重置；2）图链式思维（GCOT）重规划，在大语言模型上对蒸馏图进行推理，实现令牌高效的重规划。

Result: 大量仿真和真实世界实验表明，SERP在各种基准测试中实现了更高的成功率且令牌消耗更低，验证了其在多样化环境中的优越鲁棒性和效率。

Conclusion: SERP通过从冻结模型向进化模型的范式转变，实现了更智能、更高效的重规划，为复杂环境中的具身导航提供了更强大的失败恢复能力。

Abstract: Failure is inevitable for embodied navigation in complex environments. To enhance the resilience, replanning (RP) is a viable option, where the robot is allowed to fail, but is capable of adjusting plan until success. However, existing RP approaches freeze the ego action model and miss the opportunities to explore better plans by upgrading the robot itself. To address this limitation, we propose Self-Evolutionary RePlanning, or SERP for short, which leads to a paradigm shift from frozen models towards evolving models by run-time learning from recent experiences. In contrast to existing model evolution approaches that often get stuck at predefined static parameters, we introduce agentic self-evolving action model that uses in-context learning with auto-differentiation (ILAD) for adaptive function adjustment and global parameter reset. To achieve token-efficient replanning for SERP, we also propose graph chain-of-thought (GCOT) replanning with large language model (LLM) inference over distilled graphs. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that SERP achieves higher success rate with lower token expenditure over various benchmarks, validating its superior robustness and efficiency across diverse environments.

</details>


### [137] [SPARC: Spatial-Aware Path Planning via Attentive Robot Communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02845)
*Sayang Mu,Xiangyu Wu,Bo An*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出RMHA通信机制，通过显式嵌入曼哈顿距离来增强多机器人路径规划中的注意力权重计算，使机器人能动态优先处理空间相关邻居的消息，在拥挤区域显著提升协调效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有学习通信方法将所有相邻机器人同等对待，忽略了空间邻近性，导致在拥挤区域（协调最关键的地方）注意力被稀释。需要一种能根据空间相关性动态调整通信优先级的机制。

Method: 提出关系增强多头注意力（RMHA）：1）在注意力权重计算中显式嵌入成对曼哈顿距离；2）使用距离约束注意力掩码；3）GRU门控消息融合；4）与MAPPO无缝集成进行稳定端到端训练。

Result: 在从8个训练机器人到128个测试机器人的零样本泛化中（40x40网格，30%障碍密度），RMHA达到约75%成功率，比最佳基线高出25个百分点以上。消融研究证实距离关系编码是高密度环境下成功率提升的关键因素。

Conclusion: RMHA通过显式编码空间距离关系，有效解决了多机器人路径规划中通信注意力稀释问题，在拥挤环境下显著提升了协调效率和成功率，为密集多机器人系统的通信优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Efficient communication is critical for decentralized Multi-Robot Path Planning (MRPP), yet existing learned communication methods treat all neighboring robots equally regardless of their spatial proximity, leading to diluted attention in congested regions where coordination matters most. We propose Relation enhanced Multi Head Attention (RMHA), a communication mechanism that explicitly embeds pairwise Manhattan distances into the attention weight computation, enabling each robot to dynamically prioritize messages from spatially relevant neighbors. Combined with a distance-constrained attention mask and GRU gated message fusion, RMHA integrates seamlessly with MAPPO for stable end-to-end training. In zero-shot generalization from 8 training robots to 128 test robots on 40x40 grids, RMHA achieves approximately 75 percent success rate at 30 percent obstacle density outperforming the best baseline by over 25 percentage points. Ablation studies confirm that distance-relation encoding is the key contributor to success rate improvement in high-density environments. Index Terms-Multi-robot path planning, graph attention mechanism, multi-head attention, communication optimization, cooperative decision-making

</details>


### [138] [The Six Hug Commandments: Design and Evaluation of a Human-Sized Hugging Robot with Visual and Haptic Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2101.07679)
*Alexis E. Block,Sammy Christen,Roger Gassert,Otmar Hilliges,Katherine J. Kuchenbecker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出了机器人拥抱的六个原则，并基于这些原则开发了HuggieBot 2.0平台，通过实验验证了触觉反应性对提升用户体验的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会接触（如拥抱）对人类福祉至关重要，缺乏社会接触会对个人健康产生负面影响。现有机器人拥抱技术未能充分满足自然愉悦拥抱的需求，需要建立系统性的设计原则来改进机器人拥抱体验。

Method: 提出机器人拥抱的六个原则（柔软、温暖、人体尺寸、视觉感知、适应调整、可靠释放），基于这些原则开发HuggieBot 2.0平台，该平台具有柔软温暖的充气身体（HuggieChest），并使用视觉和触觉传感实现闭环拥抱控制。通过在线视频观看研究（117名用户）和现场实验（32名用户，每人进行8次拥抱）验证平台效果。

Result: 在线研究显示HuggieBot 2.0比基于PR2的1.0版本更具吸引力。现场实验表明，增加触觉反应性显著改善了用户对拥抱机器人的感知，验证了四个新原则的有效性，并揭示了进一步改进的机会。

Conclusion: 提出的六个机器人拥抱原则是有效的设计指南，触觉反应性是提升机器人拥抱体验的关键因素。HuggieBot 2.0平台展示了这些原则的实际应用价值，为未来社会机器人交互研究提供了重要基础。

Abstract: Receiving a hug is one of the best ways to feel socially supported, and the lack of social touch can have severe negative effects on an individual's well-being. Based on previous research both within and outside of HRI, we propose six tenets ("commandments") of natural and enjoyable robotic hugging: a hugging robot should be soft, be warm, be human sized, visually perceive its user, adjust its embrace to the user's size and position, and reliably release when the user wants to end the hug. Prior work validated the first two tenets, and the final four are new. We followed all six tenets to create a new robotic platform, HuggieBot 2.0, that has a soft, warm, inflated body (HuggieChest) and uses visual and haptic sensing to deliver closed-loop hugging. We first verified the outward appeal of this platform in comparison to the previous PR2-based HuggieBot 1.0 via an online video-watching study involving 117 users. We then conducted an in-person experiment in which 32 users each exchanged eight hugs with HuggieBot 2.0, experiencing all combinations of visual hug initiation, haptic sizing, and haptic releasing. The results show that adding haptic reactivity definitively improves user perception a hugging robot, largely verifying our four new tenets and illuminating several interesting opportunities for further improvement.

</details>


### [139] [Design, Modeling and Direction Control of a Wire-Driven Robotic Fish Based on a 2-DoF Crank-Slider Mechanism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02851)
*Yita Wang,Chen Chen,Yicheng Chen,Jinjie Li,Yuichi Motegi,Kenji Ohkuma,Toshihiro Maki,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种采用2自由度曲柄滑块机构的线驱动机器鱼，通过解耦推进与转向，实现了高速游动和敏捷机动性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BCF模式机器鱼存在推进与机动性的权衡：多伺服电机方案游速有限，而单无刷电机方案机动性差、难以平滑转向。需要一种能同时实现高速游动和敏捷机动的解决方案。

Method: 设计了弹性骨架、防水结构和2自由度曲柄滑块驱动机构，实现推进与转向的解耦；建立了驱动建模和身体动力学模型；提出了前馈-反馈组合控制策略，实现推进和转向的独立调节。

Result: 通过原型实验验证了设计、建模和控制方法的可行性，展示了机器鱼的游动、转向和方向控制能力，实现了高速游动与敏捷机动的平衡。

Conclusion: 提出的2自由度曲柄滑块线驱动机器鱼成功解决了传统BCF机器鱼在游速与机动性之间的权衡问题，为环境监测和生物调查应用提供了兼具高速游动和敏捷机动能力的解决方案。

Abstract: Robotic fish have attracted growing attention in recent years owing to their biomimetic design and potential applications in environmental monitoring and biological surveys. Among robotic fish employing the Body-Caudal Fin (BCF) locomotion pattern, motor-driven actuation is widely adopted. Some approaches utilize multiple servo motors to achieve precise body curvature control, while others employ a brushless motor to drive the tail via wire or rod, enabling higher oscillation and swimming speeds. However, the former approaches typically result in limited swimming speed, whereas the latter suffer from poor maneuverability, with few capable of smooth turning. To address this trade-off, we develop a wire-driven robotic fish equipped with a 2-degree-of-freedom (DoF) crank-slider mechanism that decouples propulsion from steering, enabling both high swimming speed and agile maneuvering. In this paper, we first present the design of the robotic fish, including the elastic skeleton, waterproof structure, and the actuation mechanism that realizes the decoupling. We then establish the actuation modeling and body dynamics to analyze the locomotion behavior. Furthermore, we propose a combined feedforward-feedback control strategy to achieve independent regulation of propulsion and steering. Finally, we validate the feasibility of the design, modeling, and control through a series of prototype experiments, demonstrating swimming, turning, and directional control.

</details>


### [140] [In the Arms of a Robot: Designing Autonomous Hugging Robots with Intra-Hug Gestures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2202.09935)
*Alexis E. Block,Hasti Seifi,Otmar Hilliges,Roger Gassert,Katherine J. Kuchenbecker*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该论文提出了六项设计交互式拥抱机器人的新指南，通过两个研究验证了这些指南，开发了能够感知人类拥抱手势并做出响应的机器人系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拥抱是复杂的情感互动，通常包含挤压等手势。现有拥抱机器人缺乏对拥抱过程中人类手势的感知和响应能力，需要设计更自然、智能的交互式拥抱机器人。

Method: 1. 提出六项设计交互式拥抱机器人的新指南；2. 开发HuggieBot 2.0机器人，通过麦克风和压力传感器收集用户拥抱手势数据；3. 训练感知算法分类四种人类拥抱手势（保持、摩擦、轻拍、挤压），准确率达88%；4. 基于用户评分创建概率行为算法，实时选择机器人响应；5. 升级到HuggieBot 3.0，验证手势感知系统和行为算法。

Result: 1. 32名用户与HuggieBot 2.0交换并评价了16次拥抱；2. 用户喜欢机器人的挤压回应，无论他们执行什么动作；3. 用户重视机器人响应的多样性，并欣赏机器人主动发起的拥抱内手势；4. 16名用户验证了HuggieBot 3.0，机器人响应和主动手势受到极大喜爱；5. 用户在实验后期认为机器人更自然、更愉快、更智能；6. 研究后用户感觉更被机器人理解，认为机器人拥抱更舒适。

Conclusion: 该研究成功开发了能够感知和响应人类拥抱手势的交互式机器人系统，验证了提出的设计指南。用户对机器人的主动手势和多样化响应给予积极评价，表明感知算法和行为算法有效提升了拥抱机器人的自然性、智能性和情感互动质量。

Abstract: Hugs are complex affective interactions that often include gestures like squeezes. We present six new guidelines for designing interactive hugging robots, which we validate through two studies with our custom robot. To achieve autonomy, we investigated robot responses to four human intra-hug gestures: holding, rubbing, patting, and squeezing. Thirty-two users each exchanged and rated sixteen hugs with an experimenter-controlled HuggieBot 2.0. The robot's inflated torso's microphone and pressure sensor collected data of the subjects' demonstrations that were used to develop a perceptual algorithm that classifies user actions with 88\% accuracy. Users enjoyed robot squeezes, regardless of their performed action, they valued variety in the robot response, and they appreciated robot-initiated intra-hug gestures. From average user ratings, we created a probabilistic behavior algorithm that chooses robot responses in real time. We implemented improvements to the robot platform to create HuggieBot 3.0 and then validated its gesture perception system and behavior algorithm with sixteen users. The robot's responses and proactive gestures were greatly enjoyed. Users found the robot more natural, enjoyable, and intelligent in the last phase of the experiment than in the first. After the study, they felt more understood by the robot and thought robots were nicer to hug.

</details>


### [141] [CoFL: Continuous Flow Fields for Language-Conditioned Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02854)
*Haokun Liu,Zhaoqi Ma,Yicheng Chen,Masaki Kitagawa,Wentao Zhang,Jinjie Li,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CoFL是一种端到端导航策略，直接将鸟瞰图观测和语言指令映射为连续流场，通过数值积分生成平滑轨迹，在未见场景中优于模块化VLM规划器和生成式策略基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有语言条件导航方法依赖脆弱的模块化组件或成本高昂的动作序列生成，需要更鲁棒、高效的端到端解决方案。

Method: 提出CoFL端到端策略，将BEV观测和语言指令映射到连续流场，输出任意2D投影位置的瞬时速度，通过数值积分获得轨迹；构建包含50万+BEV图像-指令对的数据集，基于Matterport3D和ScanNet的BEV语义图进行流程标注。

Result: CoFL在严格未见场景中显著优于模块化VLM规划器和生成式策略基线；在真实世界实验中实现零样本部署，保持可靠的闭环控制和高成功率。

Conclusion: CoFL通过连续流场表示提供了一种有效的端到端导航方法，克服了模块化系统和离散动作生成的局限性，在仿真和真实环境中都表现出色。

Abstract: Language-conditioned navigation pipelines often rely on brittle modular components or costly action-sequence generation. To address these limitations, we present CoFL, an end-to-end policy that directly maps a bird's-eye view (BEV) observation and a language instruction to a continuous flow field for navigation. Instead of predicting discrete action tokens or sampling action chunks via iterative denoising, CoFL outputs instantaneous velocities that can be queried at arbitrary 2D projected locations. Trajectories are obtained by numerical integration of the predicted field, producing smooth motion that remains reactive under closed-loop execution. To enable large-scale training, we build a dataset of over 500k BEV image-instruction pairs, each procedurally annotated with a flow field and a trajectory derived from BEV semantic maps built on Matterport3D and ScanNet. By training on a mixed distribution, CoFL significantly outperforms modular Vision-Language Model (VLM)-based planners and generative policy baselines on strictly unseen scenes. Finally, we deploy CoFL zero-shot in real-world experiments with overhead BEV observations across multiple layouts, maintaining reliable closed-loop control and a high success rate.

</details>


### [142] [Rhythm: Learning Interactive Whole-Body Control for Dual Humanoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02856)
*Hongjin Chen,Wei Zhang,Pengfei Li,Shihao Ma,Ke Ma,Yujie Jin,Zijun Xu,Xiaohui Wang,Yupeng Zheng,Zining Wang,Jieru Zhao,Yilun Chen,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Rhythm框架首次实现双人形机器人实时交互全身控制，通过交互感知运动重定向、交互引导强化学习和现实部署系统，成功将拥抱、跳舞等复杂行为从仿真转移到真实机器人。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现多人形机器人系统的交互式全身控制对于解锁共享环境中复杂协作能力至关重要。虽然单个机器人敏捷性已有显著提升，但由于严重的运动学不匹配和复杂接触动力学，实现物理耦合的多机器人交互仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出Rhythm统一框架，包含三个核心组件：1) 交互感知运动重定向模块，从人类数据生成可行的人形交互参考；2) 交互引导强化学习策略，通过基于图的奖励掌握耦合动力学；3) 现实部署系统，实现双人形交互的鲁棒转移。

Result: 在物理Unitree G1机器人上进行广泛实验，证明该框架实现了鲁棒的交互式全身控制，成功将拥抱、跳舞等多种行为从仿真转移到现实。

Conclusion: Rhythm是首个实现双人形机器人系统在现实世界中部署的统一框架，能够处理复杂的物理合理交互，为多人形机器人协作开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Realizing interactive whole-body control for multi-humanoid systems is critical for unlocking complex collaborative capabilities in shared environments. Although recent advancements have significantly enhanced the agility of individual robots, bridging the gap to physically coupled multi-humanoid interaction remains challenging, primarily due to severe kinematic mismatches and complex contact dynamics. To address this, we introduce Rhythm, the first unified framework enabling real-world deployment of dual-humanoid systems for complex, physically plausible interactions. Our framework integrates three core components: (1) an Interaction-Aware Motion Retargeting (IAMR) module that generates feasible humanoid interaction references from human data; (2) an Interaction-Guided Reinforcement Learning (IGRL) policy that masters coupled dynamics via graph-based rewards; and (3) a real-world deployment system that enables robust transfer of dual-humanoid interaction. Extensive experiments on physical Unitree G1 robots demonstrate that our framework achieves robust interactive whole-body control, successfully transferring diverse behaviors such as hugging and dancing from simulation to reality.

</details>


### [143] [Emerging trends in Cislunar Space for Lunar Science Exploration and Space Robotics aiding Human Spaceflight Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02878)
*Arsalan Muhammad,Yue Wang,Hai Huang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了月球作为地外测试平台在推动人类持续存在和星际探索中的关键作用，重点关注人工智能与空间机器人技术在月球科学探索和载人航天任务中的变革性潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 月球作为独特的地外测试平台，对于推进尖端科技和科学研究至关重要，这些研究是实现月球表面持续人类存在和支持未来星际探索的关键。研究旨在识别和调查具有重大变革潜力的关键研究领域，以加速人类星际探索的雄心。

Method: 研究识别并深入分析了两个关键研究领域：1) 人工智能与空间机器人技术在月球科学探索中的应用；2) 空间机器人技术在载人登月任务中的支持作用，包括基础设施预部署、原位资源利用、表面操作支持和宇航员安全保障。

Result: 研究表明，通过整合自主性、机器学习和实时传感器融合技术，空间机器人不仅能增强人类能力，还能作为实现可持续月球探索的"力量倍增器"，为未来的火星及更远载人任务铺平道路。

Conclusion: 人工智能和空间机器人技术是推动月球探索和未来星际任务的关键变革力量，它们通过重新定义太空探索边界、支持载人航天任务基础设施和操作，为实现可持续的月球存在和更远深空探索提供了技术基础。

Abstract: In recent years, the Moon has emerged as an unparalleled extraterrestrial testbed for advancing cuttingedge technological and scientific research critical to enabling sustained human presence on its surface and supporting future interplanetary exploration. This study identifies and investigates two pivotal research domains with substantial transformative potential for accelerating humanity interplanetary aspirations. First is Lunar Science Exploration with Artificial Intelligence and Space Robotics which focusses on AI and Space Robotics redefining the frontiers of space exploration. Second being Space Robotics aid in manned spaceflight to the Moon serving as critical assets for pre-deployment infrastructure development, In-Situ Resource Utilization, surface operations support, and astronaut safety assurance. By integrating autonomy, machine learning, and realtime sensor fusion, space robotics not only augment human capabilities but also serve as force multipliers in achieving sustainable lunar exploration, paving the way for future crewed missions to Mars and beyond.

</details>


### [144] [Tracing Back Error Sources to Explain and Mitigate Pose Estimation Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02881)
*Loris Schneider,Yitian Shi,Rosa Wolf,Carolin Brenner,Rudolph Triebel,Rania Rayyes*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出模块化、不确定性感知的位姿估计框架，通过错误检测、归因和针对性恢复提升ICP的鲁棒性，在机器人抓取任务中实现与基础模型相当的性能，同时保持简单快速


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基础通用位姿估计器试图用单一模型处理多种误差源，但难以应对环境不确定性，且推理时间长、计算量大。需要更高效、鲁棒的解决方案

Method: 提出模块化、不确定性感知的框架：1) 检测位姿估计失败；2) 将误差归因到特定误差源；3) 仅在必要时应用针对性缓解策略。以ICP作为简单轻量级位姿估计器实例化该框架

Result: 显著提升了ICP的鲁棒性，在真实世界机器人抓取任务中实现了与基础模型竞争的性能，同时依赖更简单、更快速的位姿估计器

Conclusion: 模块化、不确定性感知的框架通过错误检测、归因和针对性恢复，能够显著提升简单位姿估计器的鲁棒性，为机器人操作任务提供高效可靠的解决方案

Abstract: Robust estimation of object poses in robotic manipulation is often addressed using foundational general estimators, that aim to handle diverse error sources naively within a single model. Still, they struggle due to environmental uncertainties, while requiring long inference times and heavy computation. In contrast, we propose a modular, uncertainty-aware framework that attributes pose estimation errors to specific error sources and applies targeted mitigation strategies only when necessary. Instantiated with Iterative Closest Point (ICP) as a simple and lightweight pose estimator, we leverage our framework for real-world robotic grasping tasks. By decomposing pose estimation into failure detection, error attribution, and targeted recovery, we significantly improve the robustness of ICP and achieve competitive performance compared to foundation models, while relying on a substantially simpler and faster pose estimator.

</details>


### [145] [Architectural HRI: Towards a Robotic Paradigm Shift in Human-Building Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03052)
*Alex Binh Vinh Duc Nguyen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 论文提出人-建筑交互(HBI)正从适应性建筑服务扩展到建筑空间的物理驱动，这代表了HBI的范式转变，也为将人机交互知识转移到非常规机器人形态提供了机会。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感、通信、界面、控制和机器人技术的进步正在推动人-建筑交互(HBI)超越传统的适应性建筑服务和立面，向建筑空间的物理驱动发展。同时，机器人家具、群体机器人和形状可变空间的研究表明，建筑元素现在可以通过机器人增强来移动、重新配置和适应空间。这些发展为HBI带来了范式转变的潜力。

Method: 论文采用概念性分析和跨学科综合的方法，通过整合HCI、环境心理学、认知科学和建筑学等领域的知识，提出将人机交互(HRI)知识转移到非常规机器人形态（包括将机器人视为多个建筑层甚至整个建筑）的研究框架。

Result: 论文识别出建筑人机交互面临的挑战：建筑HRI在时间、空间和社会维度上的复杂性，以及跨学科知识的碎片化。这些挑战阻碍了建筑形式中机器人驱动的统一研究。

Conclusion: 作者呼吁开展跨学科研究，统一建筑形式中机器人驱动的"为什么"（目的）、"是什么"（内容）和"如何做"（方法），以应对建筑HRI的复杂性挑战，实现多个建筑层同步适应居住者需求和可持续性目标的整体支持。

Abstract: Recent advances in sensing, communication, interfaces, control, and robotics are expanding Human-Building Interaction (HBI) beyond adaptive building services and facades toward the physical actuation of architectural space. In parallel, research in robotic furniture, swarm robotics, and shape-changing spaces shows that architectural elements can now be robotically augmented to move, reconfigure, and adapt space. We propose that these advances promise a paradigm shift in HBI, in which multiple building layers physically adapt in synchrony to support occupant needs and sustainability goals more holistically. Conversely, we argue that this emerging paradigm also provides an ideal case for transferring HRI knowledge to unconventional robotic morphologies, including the interpretation of the robot as multiple architectural layers or even as a building. However, this research agenda remains challenged by the temporal, spatial, and social complexity of architectural HRI, and by fragmented knowledge across HCI, environmental psychology, cognitive science, and architecture. We therefore call for interdisciplinary research that unifies the why, what, and how of robotic actuation in architectural forms.

</details>


### [146] [Self-supervised Domain Adaptation for Visual 3D Pose Estimation of Nano-drone Racing Gates by Enforcing Geometric Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02936)
*Nicholas Carlotti,Michele Antonazzi,Elia Cereda,Mirko Nava,Nicola Basilico,Daniele Palossi,Alessandro Giusti*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用预训练CNN进行无人机竞速门相对姿态估计，通过无监督域适应方法利用真实飞行数据提升模型性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决无人机竞速门姿态估计中的仿真到现实差距问题，预训练模型在真实环境中性能下降，需要适应目标域

Method: 提出无监督域适应方法：使用无人机沿任意轨迹飞行采集的真实图像序列，以机载传感器测量的里程计作为自监督标注，通过状态一致性损失确保不同时间采集的图像预测的姿态与里程计一致

Result: 方法优于其他最先进的UDA方法，位置平均绝对误差（x=26, y=28, z=10 cm），方向误差（ψ=13°），相比基线位置提升40%，方向提升37%，仅需10分钟真实飞行数据，推理时间30.4ms（33 fps）

Conclusion: 提出的无监督域适应方法能有效缩小仿真到现实差距，仅需少量真实数据即可显著提升无人机竞速门姿态估计性能，满足实时部署需求

Abstract: We consider the task of visually estimating the relative pose of a drone racing gate in front of a nano-quadrotor, using a convolutional neural network pre-trained on simulated data to regress the gate's pose. Due to the sim-to-real gap, the pre-trained model underperforms in the real world and must be adapted to the target domain. We propose an unsupervised domain adaptation (UDA) approach using only real image sequences collected by the drone flying an arbitrary trajectory in front of a gate; sequences are annotated in a self-supervised fashion with the drone's odometry as measured by its onboard sensors. On this dataset, a state consistency loss enforces that two images acquired at different times yield pose predictions that are consistent with the drone's odometry. Results indicate that our approach outperforms other SoA UDA approaches, has a low mean absolute error in position (x=26, y=28, z=10 cm) and orientation ($ψ$=13${^{\circ}}$), an improvement of 40% in position and 37% in orientation over a baseline. The approach's effectiveness is appreciable with as few as 10 minutes of real-world flight data and yields models with an inference time of 30.4ms (33 fps) when deployed aboard the Crazyflie 2.1 Brushless nano-drone.

</details>


### [147] [DreamFlow: Local Navigation Beyond Observation via Conditional Flow Matching in the Latent Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02976)
*Jiwon Park,Dongkyu Lee,I Made Aswin Nahrendra,Jaeyoung Lim,Hyun Myung*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DreamFlow：基于深度强化学习的局部导航框架，通过条件流匹配扩展机器人感知范围，预测未观测环境特征以主动避免局部最小值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统局部规划器依赖启发式方法，在密集障碍物环境中容易失败；基于深度强化学习的方法具有适应性，但受限于有限的机载感知能力，无法感知视野外的环境结构，导致导航失败

Method: 提出DreamFlow框架，使用条件流匹配(CFM)扩展机器人感知范围。CFM预测模块学习局部高度图潜在表示与更广泛空间表示之间的概率映射，该映射以导航上下文为条件，使导航策略能够预测未观测环境特征

Result: 实验结果表明，DreamFlow在潜在预测准确性和导航性能方面优于现有方法。该方法在四足机器人上进一步验证，在杂乱的真实世界环境中表现良好

Conclusion: DreamFlow通过条件流匹配扩展感知范围，使机器人能够预测未观测环境特征并主动避免局部最小值，在密集障碍物环境中显著提升了导航性能

Abstract: Local navigation in cluttered environments often suffers from dense obstacles and frequent local minima. Conventional local planners rely on heuristics and are prone to failure, while deep reinforcement learning(DRL)based approaches provide adaptability but are constrained by limited onboard sensing. These limitations lead to navigation failures because the robot cannot perceive structures outside its field of view. In this paper, we propose DreamFlow, a DRL-based local navigation framework that extends the robot's perceptual horizon through conditional flow matching(CFM). The proposed CFM based prediction module learns probabilistic mapping between local height map latent representation and broader spatial representation conditioned on navigation context. This enables the navigation policy to predict unobserved environmental features and proactively avoid potential local minima. Experimental results demonstrate that DreamFlow outperforms existing methods in terms of latent prediction accuracy and navigation performance in simulation. The proposed method was further validated in cluttered real world environments with a quadrupedal robot. The project page is available at https://dreamflow-icra.github.io.

</details>


### [148] [CASSR: Continuous A-Star Search through Reachability for real time footstep planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.02989)
*Jiayi Wang,Steve Tonneau*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CASSR框架通过递归传播机器人运动学约束的凸连续公式，结合EPA启发式算法，实现高效足部步态规划，在125ms内规划30步，比传统方法快100倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统A*方法需要离散化可达性约束，而混合整数规划（MIP）虽然支持连续公式但计算复杂度高，特别是包含旋转时。需要一种既能处理连续约束又高效的足部步态规划方法。

Method: 提出CASSR框架，在A*搜索中递归传播机器人运动学约束的凸连续公式，结合基于EPA算法的新成本启发式函数，实现高效规划。

Result: CASSR能在125毫秒内规划多达30个足部步态，比传统离散化A*快100倍，并超越商业MIP求解器，实现快速可靠的实时足部步态规划。

Conclusion: CASSR为双足机器人提供了快速、可靠且实时的足部步态规划解决方案，通过连续约束公式和高效启发式算法显著提升了规划性能。

Abstract: Footstep planning involves a challenging combinatorial search. Traditional A* approaches require discretising reachability constraints, while Mixed-Integer Programming (MIP) supports continuous formulations but quickly becomes intractable, especially when rotations are included. We present CASSR, a novel framework that recursively propagates convex, continuous formulations of a robot's kinematic constraints within an A* search. Combined with a new cost-to-go heuristic based on the EPA algorithm, CASSR efficiently plans contact sequences of up to 30 footsteps in under 125 ms. Experiments on biped locomotion tasks demonstrate that CASSR outperforms traditional discretised A* by up to a factor of 100, while also surpassing a commercial MIP solver. These results show that CASSR enables fast, reliable, and real-time footstep planning for biped robots.

</details>


### [149] [MA-CoNav: A Master-Slave Multi-Agent Framework with Hierarchical Collaboration and Dual-Level Reflection for Long-Horizon Embodied VLN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03024)
*Ling Luo,Qianqian Bai*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MA-CoNav提出了一种多智能体协作导航框架，通过主从分层架构将导航任务的功能解耦分配给专门智能体，解决了传统VLN方法在复杂长距离任务中的感知失真和决策漂移问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言导航方法在复杂长距离任务中容易因单个智能体的认知过载而出现感知失真和决策漂移问题。受分布式认知理论启发，需要建立更高效的"语言理解-视觉感知-具身执行"闭环。

Method: 采用主从分层协作架构，将导航任务解耦为专门智能体：主智能体负责全局协调，从属智能体组包括观察智能体（环境描述）、规划智能体（任务分解与动态验证）、执行智能体（同时建图与行动）、记忆智能体（结构化经验管理）。引入"局部-全局"双阶段反思机制动态优化导航流程。

Result: 在Limo Pro机器人采集的真实室内数据集上进行实验，无需场景特定微调。MA-CoNav在多个指标上全面优于现有主流VLN方法。

Conclusion: MA-CoNav框架通过多智能体协作有效解决了VLN中的认知过载问题，提高了复杂长距离导航任务的性能，为分布式认知理论在具身智能中的应用提供了实践验证。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation (VLN) aims to empower robots with the ability to perform long-horizon navigation in unfamiliar environments based on complex linguistic instructions. Its success critically hinges on establishing an efficient ``language-understanding -- visual-perception -- embodied-execution'' closed loop. Existing methods often suffer from perceptual distortion and decision drift in complex, long-distance tasks due to the cognitive overload of a single agent. Inspired by distributed cognition theory, this paper proposes MA-CoNav, a Multi-Agent Collaborative Navigation framework. This framework adopts a ``Master-Slave'' hierarchical agent collaboration architecture, decoupling and distributing the perception, planning, execution, and memory functions required for navigation tasks to specialized agents. Specifically, the Master Agent is responsible for global orchestration, while the Subordinate Agent group collaborates through a clear division of labor: an Observation Agent generates environment descriptions, a Planning Agent performs task decomposition and dynamic verification, an Execution Agent handles simultaneous mapping and action, and a Memory Agent manages structured experiences. Furthermore, the framework introduces a ``Local-Global'' dual-stage reflection mechanism to dynamically optimize the entire navigation pipeline. Empirical experiments were conducted using a real-world indoor dataset collected by a Limo Pro robot, with no scene-specific fine-tuning performed on the models throughout the process. The results demonstrate that MA-CoNav comprehensively outperforms existing mainstream VLN methods across multiple metrics.

</details>


### [150] [CMoE: Contrastive Mixture of Experts for Motion Control and Terrain Adaptation of Humanoid Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03067)
*Shihao Ma,Hongjin Chen,Zijun Xu,Yi Zhao,Ke Wu,Ruichen Yang,Leyao Zou,Zhongxue Gan,Wenchao Ding*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CMoE：一种结合对比学习的单阶段强化学习框架，通过优化专家激活分布，提升人形机器人在复杂地形上的自主导航能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统混合专家框架在实际应用中存在专家激活分布均匀化的问题，导致专家专业化程度不足，限制了模型在复杂地形导航中的表达能力

Method: 提出CMoE框架，将对比学习集成到单阶段强化学习中，通过对比约束最大化同一地形内专家激活的一致性，同时最小化不同地形间的相似性，促进专家在特定地形类型上的专业化

Result: 在Unitree G1人形机器人上的实验表明，CMoE能够使机器人穿越高达20cm的连续台阶和宽达80cm的沟壑，并在多样化混合地形上实现稳健自然的步态，超越了现有方法的限制

Conclusion: CMoE通过对比学习优化专家激活分布，有效解决了混合专家框架中专家专业化不足的问题，显著提升了人形机器人在复杂地形上的导航性能，并公开代码以促进社区发展

Abstract: For effective deployment in real-world environments, humanoid robots must autonomously navigate a diverse range of complex terrains with abrupt transitions. While the Vanilla mixture of experts (MoE) framework is theoretically capable of modeling diverse terrain features, in practice, the gating network exhibits nearly uniform expert activations across different terrains, weakening the expert specialization and limiting the model's expressive power. To address this limitation, we introduce CMoE, a novel single-stage reinforcement learning framework that integrates contrastive learning to refine expert activation distributions. By imposing contrastive constraints, CMoE maximizes the consistency of expert activations within the same terrain while minimizing their similarity across different terrains, thereby encouraging experts to specialize in distinct terrain types. We validated our approach on the Unitree G1 humanoid robot through a series of challenging experiments. Results demonstrate that CMoE enables the robot to traverse continuous steps up to 20 cm high and gaps up to 80 cm wide, while achieving robust and natural gait across diverse mixed terrains, surpassing the limits of existing methods. To support further research and foster community development, we release our code publicly.

</details>


### [151] [RL-Based Coverage Path Planning for Deformable Objects on 3D Surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03137)
*Yuhang Zhang,Jinming Ma,Feng Wu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于强化学习的可变形物体表面擦拭方法，利用简化状态表示和高效特征提取，在模拟器中训练智能体生成覆盖路径，并在真实机器人上验证可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前可变形物体操作研究主要集中在折叠衣物、处理绳索等任务，涉及丰富接触的表面擦拭任务研究相对不足。人类擦拭表面时依赖视觉和触觉反馈，但现有算法面临遮挡等问题，触觉感知研究仍在发展中。使用布料或海绵覆盖表面需要精确的机器人操作能力。

Method: 使用高效易得的模拟器训练强化学习智能体执行可变形物体表面擦拭任务。采用谐波UV映射简化物体表面状态表示，处理模拟器中的接触反馈到2D特征图，使用缩放分组卷积（SGCNN）高效提取特征。智能体在降维动作空间中输出动作以生成覆盖路径。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在关键指标上优于先前方法，包括总路径长度和覆盖面积。将生成的路径部署在Kinova Gen3机械臂上，在躯干模型背部进行擦拭实验，验证了方法的可行性。

Conclusion: 提出的方法通过简化状态表示和高效特征提取，成功实现了可变形物体表面擦拭任务的强化学习训练，并在真实机器人上验证了其可行性，为解决接触丰富的可变形物体操作问题提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Currently, manipulation tasks for deformable objects often focus on activities like folding clothes, handling ropes, and manipulating bags. However, research on contact-rich tasks involving deformable objects remains relatively underdeveloped. When humans use cloth or sponges to wipe surfaces, they rely on both vision and tactile feedback. Yet, current algorithms still face challenges with issues like occlusion, while research on tactile perception for manipulation is still evolving. Tasks such as covering surfaces with deformable objects demand not only perception but also precise robotic manipulation. To address this, we propose a method that leverages efficient and accessible simulators for task execution. Specifically, we train a reinforcement learning agent in a simulator to manipulate deformable objects for surface wiping tasks. We simplify the state representation of object surfaces using harmonic UV mapping, process contact feedback from the simulator on 2D feature maps, and use scaled grouped convolutions (SGCNN) to extract features efficiently. The agent then outputs actions in a reduced-dimensional action space to generate coverage paths. Experiments demonstrate that our method outperforms previous approaches in key metrics, including total path length and coverage area. We deploy these paths on a Kinova Gen3 manipulator to perform wiping experiments on the back of a torso model, validating the feasibility of our approach.

</details>


### [152] [Look Forward to Walk Backward: Efficient Terrain Memory for Backward Locomotion with Forward Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03138)
*Shixin Luo,Songbo Li,Yuan Hao,Yaqi Wang,Jun Zheng,Jun Wu,Qiuguo Zhu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出LF2WB框架，利用前向深度相机和本体感知在前进时构建紧凑关联记忆，用于无后向视觉的避障后退运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足式机器人在后退时，前向视野无法提供预览信息，纯本体感知控制器在复杂地形上能力受限且会碰撞障碍物

Method: LF2WB框架：前进时使用前向深度和本体感知写入紧凑关联记忆，后退时检索记忆实现避障；采用delta-rule选择性更新，硬件高效并行计算训练，部署时恒定时间推理

Result: 仿真和真实场景实验证明该方法有效，在有限感知条件下提升了机器人在复杂地形上的后退敏捷性

Conclusion: LF2WB框架通过地形记忆机制，使仅配备前向深度相机的足式机器人能够在复杂地形上实现避障的后退运动

Abstract: Legged robots with egocentric forward-facing depth cameras can couple exteroception and proprioception to achieve robust forward agility on complex terrain. When these robots walk backward, the forward-only field of view provides no preview. Purely proprioceptive controllers can remain stable on moderate ground when moving backward but cannot fully exploit the robot's capabilities on complex terrain and must collide with obstacles. We present Look Forward to Walk Backward (LF2WB), an efficient terrain-memory locomotion framework that uses forward egocentric depth and proprioception to write a compact associative memory during forward motion and to retrieve it for collision-free backward locomotion without rearward vision. The memory backbone employs a delta-rule selective update that softly removes then writes the memory state along the active subspace. Training uses hardware-efficient parallel computation, and deployment runs recurrent, constant-time per-step inference with a constant-size state, making the approach suitable for onboard processors on low-cost robots. Experiments in both simulations and real-world scenarios demonstrate the effectiveness of our method, improving backward agility across complex terrains under limited sensing.

</details>


### [153] [Robotic Grasping and Placement Controlled by EEG-Based Hybrid Visual and Motor Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03181)
*Yichang Liu,Tianyu Wang,Ziyi Ye,Yawei Li,Yu-Gang Jiang,Shouyan Wang,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一个集成EEG视觉和运动想象的机器人控制框架，实现实时意图驱动的抓取和放置任务，通过零样本在线解码实现神经信号到物理控制的转换。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于脑机接口驱动的机器人技术有望增强人机交互，需要建立神经信号与物理控制之间的桥梁，实现直观的意图驱动控制。

Method: 采用离线预训练解码器的零样本在线流式处理框架，建立双通道意图接口：视觉想象识别抓取对象，运动想象确定放置姿态，使用无提示想象协议，在Base机器人平台上实现。

Result: 在线解码准确率：视觉想象40.23%，运动想象62.59%；端到端任务成功率20.88%；在遮挡目标、不同参与者姿势等多样化场景中验证了系统有效性。

Conclusion: 该系统证明高级视觉认知可以实时解码并转换为可执行的机器人命令，填补了神经信号与物理交互之间的鸿沟，验证了纯基于想象的脑机接口范式在实际人机协作中的灵活性。

Abstract: We present a framework that integrates EEG-based visual and motor imagery (VI/MI) with robotic control to enable real-time, intention-driven grasping and placement. Motivated by the promise of BCI-driven robotics to enhance human-robot interaction, this system bridges neural signals with physical control by deploying offline-pretrained decoders in a zero-shot manner within an online streaming pipeline. This establishes a dual-channel intent interface that translates visual intent into robotic actions, with VI identifying objects for grasping and MI determining placement poses, enabling intuitive control over both what to grasp and where to place. The system operates solely on EEG via a cue-free imagery protocol, achieving integration and online validation. Implemented on a Base robotic platform and evaluated across diverse scenarios, including occluded targets or varying participant postures, the system achieves online decoding accuracies of 40.23% (VI) and 62.59% (MI), with an end-to-end task success rate of 20.88%. These results demonstrate that high-level visual cognition can be decoded in real time and translated into executable robot commands, bridging the gap between neural signals and physical interaction, and validating the flexibility of a purely imagery-based BCI paradigm for practical human-robot collaboration.

</details>


### [154] [ACE-Brain-0: Spatial Intelligence as a Shared Scaffold for Universal Embodiments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03198)
*Ziyang Gong,Zehang Luo,Anke Tang,Zhe Liu,Shi Fu,Zhi Hou,Ganlin Yang,Weiyun Wang,Xiaofeng Wang,Jianbo Liu,Gen Luo,Haolan Kang,Shuang Luo,Yue Zhou,Yong Luo,Li Shen,Xiaosong Jia,Yao Mu,Xue Yang,Chunxiao Liu,Junchi Yan,Hengshuang Zhao,Dacheng Tao,Xiaogang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ACE-Brain-0是一个通用基础大脑，通过空间智能作为跨具身智能的统一支架，采用SSR范式（支架-专业化-协调）和GRPO优化，在24个空间和具身相关基准上实现SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有具身智能模型在训练统一模型处理异构具身（自动驾驶、机器人、无人机）时面临长尾数据、梯度干扰和灾难性遗忘等问题，难以平衡通用泛化与领域专精能力。

Method: 提出SSR范式：1）建立共享空间基础（空间智能作为跨具身的通用支架）；2）培养领域专业化专家；3）通过无数据模型融合协调专家。采用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)增强模型综合能力。

Result: 在24个空间和具身相关基准上，ACE-Brain-0取得了竞争性甚至最先进的性能表现。

Conclusion: 空间智能可作为跨异构具身的通用支架，SSR范式有效解决了统一模型训练中的长尾数据、梯度干扰和灾难性遗忘问题，实现了通用泛化与领域专精的平衡。

Abstract: Universal embodied intelligence demands robust generalization across heterogeneous embodiments, such as autonomous driving, robotics, and unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs). However, existing embodied brain in training a unified model over diverse embodiments frequently triggers long-tail data, gradient interference, and catastrophic forgetting, making it notoriously difficult to balance universal generalization with domain-specific proficiency. In this report, we introduce ACE-Brain-0, a generalist foundation brain that unifies spatial reasoning, autonomous driving, and embodied manipulation within a single multimodal large language model~(MLLM). Our key insight is that spatial intelligence serves as a universal scaffold across diverse physical embodiments: although vehicles, robots, and UAVs differ drastically in morphology, they share a common need for modeling 3D mental space, making spatial cognition a natural, domain-agnostic foundation for cross-embodiment transfer. Building on this insight, we propose the Scaffold-Specialize-Reconcile~(SSR) paradigm, which first establishes a shared spatial foundation, then cultivates domain-specialized experts, and finally harmonizes them through data-free model merging. Furthermore, we adopt Group Relative Policy Optimization~(GRPO) to strengthen the model's comprehensive capability. Extensive experiments demonstrate that ACE-Brain-0 achieves competitive and even state-of-the-art performance across 24 spatial and embodiment-related benchmarks.

</details>


### [155] [HoMMI: Learning Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation from Human Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03243)
*Xiaomeng Xu,Jisang Park,Han Zhang,Eric Cousineau,Aditya Bhat,Jose Barreiros,Dian Wang,Shuran Song*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HoMMI是一个从无机器人人类演示中学习全身移动操作的数据收集和策略学习框架，通过增强的UMI接口和跨具身策略设计解决观察与动作空间的具身差距问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法难以直接从无机器人的人类演示中学习需要全身协调、导航和主动感知的长时程移动操作任务，主要障碍是人类与机器人之间在观察和动作空间上的具身差距。

Method: 1) 在UMI接口上增加自我中心感知以捕获移动操作所需的全局上下文；2) 提出跨具身手眼策略设计，包括具身无关的视觉表示、松弛的头部动作表示，以及通过协调全身运动在机器人特定物理约束下实现手眼轨迹的全身控制器。

Result: 该框架能够实现需要双手和全身协调、导航和主动感知的长时程移动操作任务，实现了从无机器人人类演示到机器人策略的有效迁移。

Conclusion: HoMMI通过增强的感知和跨具身策略设计成功解决了从无机器人人类演示中学习全身移动操作的挑战，为可扩展的机器人学习提供了有效框架。

Abstract: We present Whole-Body Mobile Manipulation Interface (HoMMI), a data collection and policy learning framework that learns whole-body mobile manipulation directly from robot-free human demonstrations. We augment UMI interfaces with egocentric sensing to capture the global context required for mobile manipulation, enabling portable, robot-free, and scalable data collection. However, naively incorporating egocentric sensing introduces a larger human-to-robot embodiment gap in both observation and action spaces, making policy transfer difficult. We explicitly bridge this gap with a cross-embodiment hand-eye policy design, including an embodiment agnostic visual representation; a relaxed head action representation; and a whole-body controller that realizes hand-eye trajectories through coordinated whole-body motion under robot-specific physical constraints. Together, these enable long-horizon mobile manipulation tasks requiring bimanual and whole-body coordination, navigation, and active perception. Results are best viewed on: https://hommi-robot.github.io

</details>


### [156] [Tether: Autonomous Functional Play with Correspondence-Driven Trajectory Warping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03278)
*William Liang,Sam Wang,Hung-Ju Wang,Osbert Bastani,Yecheng Jason Ma,Dinesh Jayaraman*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Tether方法通过语义关键点对应实现数据高效的开放环策略，结合视觉语言模型指导的自主功能游戏循环，从少量演示生成多样化高质量数据，持续改进闭环模仿策略性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决机器人交互学习中的两大挑战：需要能处理多样化、分布外环境状态的鲁棒策略，以及持续产生有用机器人经验的程序，以替代劳动密集型人工演示。

Method: 1. 设计基于语义关键点对应的开放环策略，将少量源演示动作通过关键点对应变形到目标场景；2. 通过视觉语言模型指导的连续循环（任务选择、执行、评估、改进）实现自主功能游戏。

Result: 在家庭式多物体设置中首次实现从仅少量演示开始的长时间自主多任务游戏，生成超过1000条专家级轨迹，训练出的策略性能与基于人工收集演示的策略相当。

Conclusion: Tether方法通过数据高效的开放环策略和自主功能游戏循环，能够从少量演示开始持续生成高质量数据，有效改进闭环模仿策略，为机器人交互学习提供了可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: The ability to conduct and learn from interaction and experience is a central challenge in robotics, offering a scalable alternative to labor-intensive human demonstrations. However, realizing such "play" requires (1) a policy robust to diverse, potentially out-of-distribution environment states, and (2) a procedure that continuously produces useful robot experience. To address these challenges, we introduce Tether, a method for autonomous functional play involving structured, task-directed interactions. First, we design a novel open-loop policy that warps actions from a small set of source demonstrations (<=10) by anchoring them to semantic keypoint correspondences in the target scene. We show that this design is extremely data-efficient and robust even under significant spatial and semantic variations. Second, we deploy this policy for autonomous functional play in the real world via a continuous cycle of task selection, execution, evaluation, and improvement, guided by the visual understanding capabilities of vision-language models. This procedure generates diverse, high-quality datasets with minimal human intervention. In a household-like multi-object setup, our method is the first to perform many hours of autonomous multi-task play in the real world starting from only a handful of demonstrations. This produces a stream of data that consistently improves the performance of closed-loop imitation policies over time, ultimately yielding over 1000 expert-level trajectories and training policies competitive with those learned from human-collected demonstrations.

</details>


### [157] [ULTRA: Unified Multimodal Control for Autonomous Humanoid Whole-Body Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.03279)
*Xialin He,Sirui Xu,Xinyao Li,Runpei Dong,Liuyu Bian,Yu-Xiong Wang,Liang-Yan Gui*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ULTRA框架通过物理驱动的神经重定向和统一多模态控制器，实现从稀疏意图到全身运动操作的自主生成，无需测试时参考运动


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人形机器人全身运动操作自主性的核心障碍：现有方法受限于重定向数据稀缺、难以扩展到大规模技能库、依赖预定义运动参考而非从感知和高级任务规范生成行为

Method: 提出ULTRA统一框架：1) 物理驱动的神经重定向算法，将大规模运动捕捉数据转换到人形机器人形态；2) 学习统一多模态控制器，支持密集参考和稀疏任务规范，处理从精确运动捕捉状态到嘈杂自我中心视觉输入的感知

Result: 在仿真和真实Unitree G1人形机器人上评估，ULTRA能够从自我中心感知实现自主、目标条件的全身运动操作，始终优于仅跟踪的基线方法

Conclusion: ULTRA框架通过结合物理重定向、统一控制器和强化学习微调，实现了从稀疏意图生成协调全身行为的能力，为人形机器人的实际应用提供了重要进展

Abstract: Achieving autonomous and versatile whole-body loco-manipulation remains a central barrier to making humanoids practically useful. Yet existing approaches are fundamentally constrained: retargeted data are often scarce or low-quality; methods struggle to scale to large skill repertoires; and, most importantly, they rely on tracking predefined motion references rather than generating behavior from perception and high-level task specifications. To address these limitations, we propose ULTRA, a unified framework with two key components. First, we introduce a physics-driven neural retargeting algorithm that translates large-scale motion capture to humanoid embodiments while preserving physical plausibility for contact-rich interactions. Second, we learn a unified multimodal controller that supports both dense references and sparse task specifications, under sensing ranging from accurate motion-capture state to noisy egocentric visual inputs. We distill a universal tracking policy into this controller, compress motor skills into a compact latent space, and apply reinforcement learning finetuning to expand coverage and improve robustness under out-of-distribution scenarios. This enables coordinated whole-body behavior from sparse intent without test-time reference motions. We evaluate ULTRA in simulation and on a real Unitree G1 humanoid.
